ARQUIVO COMPLETO: .\firestore.rules
CAMINHO: C:\Users\glaub\palpitaco\firestore.rules
########################################################################################################################
00001: rules_version = '2';
00002: service cloud.firestore {
00003:   match /databases/{database}/documents {
00004: 
00005:     /* =========================================================
00006:        HELPERS
00007:     ========================================================= */
00008: 
00009:     function signedIn() {
00010:       return request.auth != null;
00011:     }
00012: 
00013:     // Admin = existe doc /admins/{uid} e active == true
00014:     function isAdmin() {
00015:       return signedIn()
00016:         && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/admins/$(request.auth.uid))
00017:         && get(/databases/$(database)/documents/admins/$(request.auth.uid)).data.active == true;
00018:     }
00019: 
00020:     function isOwner(uid) {
00021:       return signedIn() && request.auth.uid == uid;
00022:     }
00023: 
00024:     /**
00025:      * ✅ Campos permitidos para o PRÓPRIO usuário (client)
00026:      * - inclui variações antigas para compatibilidade
00027:      */
00028:     function allowedUserKeys() {
00029:       return [
00030:         // identity/basic
00031:         "uid",
00032:         "email",
00033:         "createdAt",
00034:         "updatedAt",
00035: 
00036:         // profile
00037:         "name",
00038:         "phoneDigits",
00039:         "phone",        // compat legado
00040:         "photoUrl",
00041:         "photoURL",     // compat legado
00042: 
00043:         // trial (compat + atual)
00044:         "trialStartAt",
00045:         "trialEndAt",
00046:         "trialActive",
00047:         "trialStart",
00048:         "trialEnd",
00049:         "trialEndsAt",
00050: 
00051:         // plan (mas com regras abaixo)
00052:         "plan"
00053:       ];
00054:     }
00055: 
00056:     /**
00057:      * ✅ No CREATE, o doc inteiro precisa estar dentro do allowlist
00058:      */
00059:     function createKeysAreSafe() {
00060:       return request.resource.data.keys().hasOnly(allowedUserKeys());
00061:     }
00062: 
00063:     /**
00064:      * ✅ No UPDATE, só pode MUDAR campos dentro do allowlist
00065:      */
00066:     function updateKeysAreSafe() {
00067:       return request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).changedKeys()
00068:         .hasOnly(allowedUserKeys());
00069:     }
00070: 
00071:     /**
00072:      * ✅ PLAN: regras seguras
00073:      * - CREATE: só aceita FREE/TRIAL (ou sem plan)
00074:      * - UPDATE: permite qualquer plan EXISTENTE (FREE/TRIAL/PRO/VIP),
00075:      *           mas o usuário NÃO pode ALTERAR o plan.
00076:      */
00077:     function planSafeForCreate() {
00078:       return !("plan" in request.resource.data)
00079:         || request.resource.data.plan in ["FREE", "TRIAL"];
00080:     }
00081: 
00082:     function planNotChangedOnUpdate() {
00083:       return !("plan" in request.resource.data)
00084:         || resource.data.plan == request.resource.data.plan;
00085:     }
00086: 
00087:     /**
00088:      * ✅ TRIAL: usuário pode setar no CREATE (porque seu client cria trial),
00089:      * mas NÃO pode alterar depois (somente admin).
00090:      */
00091:     function trialNotChangedOnUpdate() {
00092:       return
00093:         (!("trialStartAt" in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.trialStartAt == resource.data.trialStartAt) &&
00094:         (!("trialEndAt"   in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.trialEndAt   == resource.data.trialEndAt) &&
00095:         (!("trialActive"  in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.trialActive  == resource.data.trialActive) &&
00096:         (!("trialStart"   in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.trialStart   == resource.data.trialStart) &&
00097:         (!("trialEnd"     in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.trialEnd     == resource.data.trialEnd) &&
00098:         (!("trialEndsAt"  in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.trialEndsAt  == resource.data.trialEndsAt);
00099:     }
00100: 
00101:     function userCreateIsSafe() {
00102:       return createKeysAreSafe() && planSafeForCreate();
00103:     }
00104: 
00105:     function userUpdateIsSafe() {
00106:       return updateKeysAreSafe() && planNotChangedOnUpdate() && trialNotChangedOnUpdate();
00107:     }
00108: 
00109:     /* =========================================================
00110:        ADMINS
00111:     ========================================================= */
00112: 
00113:     match /admins/{uid} {
00114:       allow get: if isOwner(uid) || isAdmin();
00115:       allow list: if isAdmin();
00116:       allow create, update, delete: if false; // somente via Console (por enquanto)
00117:     }
00118: 
00119:     /* =========================================================
00120:        USERS
00121:     ========================================================= */
00122: 
00123:     match /users/{uid} {
00124:       allow get: if isOwner(uid) || isAdmin();
00125:       allow list: if isAdmin();
00126: 
00127:       // ✅ usuário pode criar o próprio doc (primeiro login/primeiro salvar)
00128:       allow create: if isOwner(uid) && userCreateIsSafe();
00129: 
00130:       // ✅ usuário pode atualizar perfil com segurança (sem mexer plan/trial)
00131:       allow update: if (isOwner(uid) && userUpdateIsSafe()) || isAdmin();
00132: 
00133:       // delete: só admin
00134:       allow delete: if isAdmin();
00135:     }
00136: 
00137:     /* =========================================================
00138:        DADOS DO APP (PREVIEW PUBLIC READ)
00139:        - Para o modo "Entrar sem login (Preview)" funcionar,
00140:          precisamos permitir READ sem auth.
00141:        - WRITE continua restrito a admin.
00142:     ========================================================= */
00143: 
00144:     match /draws/{drawId} {
00145:       allow read: if true;        // ✅ público (Preview funciona)
00146:       allow write: if isAdmin();
00147:     }
00148: 
00149:     match /draws/{drawId}/prizes/{prizeId} {
00150:       allow read: if true;        // ✅ público (Preview funciona)
00151:       allow write: if isAdmin();
00152:     }
00153: 
00154:     /* =========================================================
00155:        HISTÓRICO GLOBAL DO TOP3
00156:        - leitura pública;
00157:        - criação somente por usuário autenticado;
00158:        - snapshot original imutável;
00159:        - atualização limitada à validação do resultado.
00160:     ========================================================= */
00161: 
00162:     match /top3_predictions/{predictionId} {
00163:       allow read: if true;
00164: 
00165:       allow create: if signedIn()
00166:         && request.resource.data.createdBy == request.auth.uid
00167:         && request.resource.data.status == "predicted"
00168:         && request.resource.data.targetYmd is string
00169:         && request.resource.data.targetHour is string
00170:         && request.resource.data.lotteryKey is string
00171:         && request.resource.data.snapshot is list
00172:         && request.resource.data.snapshot.size() > 0
00173:         && request.resource.data.snapshot.size() <= 3;
00174: 
00175:       allow update: if signedIn()
00176:         && request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
00177:           "resultGrupo",
00178:           "resultMilhar",
00179:           "resultAnimal",
00180:           "hitType",
00181:           "hitScore",
00182:           "hitPosition",
00183:           "matchedValue",
00184:           "validatedAt",
00185:           "validatedBy",
00186:           "updatedAt",
00187:           "status"
00188:         ])
00189:         && request.resource.data.status == "validated"
00190:         && request.resource.data.validatedBy == request.auth.uid;
00191: 
00192:       allow delete: if false;
00193:     }
00194: 
00195:     /* =========================================================
00196:        DEFAULT: FECHA TUDO
00197:     ========================================================= */
00198: 
00199:     match /{document=**} {
00200:       allow read, write: if false;
00201:     }
00202:   }
00203: }

00055:   ACCOUNT: "account",
00056:   RESULTS: "results",
00057:   TOP3: "top3",
00058:   LATE: "late",
00059:   SEARCH: "search",
00060:   PAYMENTS: "payments",
00061:   DOWNLOADS: "downloads",
00062:   CENTENAS: "centenas",
00063:   STATISTICS: "statistics",

00066: function safeReadLS(key) {
00067:   try {
00068:     return localStorage.getItem(key);
00069:   } catch {
00070:     return null;
00071:   }
00072: }
00073: 
00074: function safeWriteLS(key, value) {
00075:   try {
00076:     localStorage.setItem(key, value);
00077:   } catch {}
00078: }
00079: 
00080: function safeRemoveLS(key) {
00081:   try {
00082:     localStorage.removeItem(key);
00083:   } catch {}
00084: }
00085: 
00086: function safeParseJson(raw) {
00087:   try {
00088:     return JSON.parse(raw);
00089:   } catch {
00090:     return null;
00091:   }
00092: }
00093: 
00094: function normalizeRoute(saved) {
00095:   if (!saved) return null;
00096:   return Object.values(ROUTES).includes(saved) ? saved : null;
00097: }

00099: /**
00100:  * ✅ Resolve default/named de forma robusta
00101:  */
00102: function resolveComponent(mod, name) {
00103:   const c = mod?.default ?? mod;
00104: 
00105:   const isProbablyReactComponent =
00106:     typeof c === "function" ||
00107:     (c && typeof c === "object" && String(c.$$typeof || "").includes("react."));
00108: 
00109:   if (!isProbablyReactComponent) {
00110:     console.error(`[IMPORT INVALID] ${name} veio inválido:`, c, " | import raw:", mod);
00111:   }
00112: 
00113:   return c;
00114: }

00120: function normalizePlan(plan) {
00121:   const p = String(plan || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00122:   if (p === "VIP") return "VIP";
00123:   if (p === "PREMIUM") return "PREMIUM";
00124:   if (p === "FREE") return "FREE";
00125:   return "";
00126: }
00127: 
00128: function loadSessionObj() {
00129:   const raw = safeReadLS(ACCOUNT_SESSION_KEY);
00130:   if (!raw) return null;
00131: 
00132:   const s = String(raw || "").trim();
00133:   if (!s || !s.startsWith("{")) return null;
00134: 
00135:   const obj = safeParseJson(s);
00136:   if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return null;
00137: 
00138:   const type = String(obj.type || "").trim().toLowerCase();
00139:   const uid = String(obj.uid || "").trim();
00140:   const email = String(obj.email || "").trim().toLowerCase();
00141:   const ok = obj.ok === true;
00142:   const plan = normalizePlan(
00143:     obj.plan ??
00144:       obj.profile?.plan ??
00145:       obj.subscription?.plan ??
00146:       obj.account?.plan ??
00147:       obj.customClaims?.plan ??
00148:       obj.claims?.plan ??
00149:       obj.appData?.plan ??
00150:       obj.metadata?.plan
00151:   );
00152: 
00153:   if (!ok) return null;
00154: 
00155:   if (type === "guest") {
00156:     return {
00157:       ok: true,
00158:       type: "guest",
00159:       plan: plan || "FREE",
00160:       uid: "",
00161:       email: "",
00162:       raw: obj,
00163:     };
00164:   }
00165: 
00166:   if (type === "user" && uid) {
00167:     return {
00168:       ok: true,
00169:       type: "user",
00170:       plan: plan || "PREMIUM",
00171:       uid,
00172:       email,
00173:       raw: obj,
00174:     };
00175:   }
00176: 
00177:   if (uid || email) {
00178:     return {
00179:       ok: true,
00180:       type: "user",
00181:       plan: plan || "PREMIUM",
00182:       uid,
00183:       email,
00184:       raw: obj,
00185:     };
00186:   }
00187: 
00188:   return null;
00189: }
00190: 
00191: function getSessionKind(sess) {
00192:   const s = sess || loadSessionObj();
00193:   if (!s || s.ok !== true) return "anon";
00194:   return s.type === "guest" ? "guest" : s.type === "user" ? "user" : "anon";
00195: }
00196: 
00197: function cleanupLegacyGuestFlagIfNeeded() {
00198:   const sess = loadSessionObj();
00199:   const guestFlag = safeReadLS(LS_GUEST_ACTIVE_KEY);
00200: 
00201:   if (!guestFlag) return;
00202: 
00203:   if (sess?.type === "guest") return;
00204: 
00205:   safeRemoveLS(LS_GUEST_ACTIVE_KEY);
00206: }

00208: /* =========================
00209:    Dashboard filters (persist)
00210: ========================= */
00211: 
00212: function normalizeLoteriaInput(v) {
00213:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim();
00214:   if (!raw) return "PT_RIO";
00215: 
00216:   const key = raw
00217:     .toLowerCase()
00218:     .normalize("NFD")
00219:     .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
00220:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
00221:     .trim();
00222: 
00223:   if (key === "federal" || key === "fed" || key === "br" || key === "brasil") {
00224:     return "FEDERAL";
00225:   }
00226: 
00227:   if (key === "rj" || key === "rio" || key === "pt_rio" || key === "pt-rio") {
00228:     return "PT_RIO";
00229:   }
00230: 
00231:   const out = key
00232:     .toUpperCase()
00233:     .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, "_")
00234:     .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
00235: 
00236:   return out || "PT_RIO";
00237: }
00238: 
00239: function loteriaToLotteryKey(loteria) {
00240:   return normalizeLoteriaInput(loteria);
00241: }
00242: 
00243: function getDefaultDashboardFilters() {
00244:   return {
00245:     loteria: "PT_RIO",
00246:     mes: "Todos",
00247:     diaMes: "Todos",
00248:     diaSemana: "Todos",
00249:     horario: "Todos",
00250:     animal: "Todos",
00251:     posicao: "Todos",
00252:   };
00253: }
00254: 
00255: function loadDashboardFilters() {
00256:   const raw = safeReadLS(DASH_FILTERS_KEY);
00257:   if (!raw) return getDefaultDashboardFilters();
00258: 
00259:   const obj = safeParseJson(raw);
00260:   if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return getDefaultDashboardFilters();
00261: 
00262:   const base = getDefaultDashboardFilters();
00263:   const loteria = normalizeLoteriaInput(obj.loteria);
00264: 
00265:   const horario =
00266:     loteria === "FEDERAL"
00267:       ? obj.horario === "Todos" || obj.horario === "19h" || obj.horario === "20h"
00268:         ? obj.horario
00269:         : "Todos"
00270:       : typeof obj.horario === "string"
00271:       ? obj.horario
00272:       : base.horario;
00273: 
00274:   return {
00275:     loteria,
00276:     mes: typeof obj.mes === "string" ? obj.mes : base.mes,
00277:     diaMes: typeof obj.diaMes === "string" ? obj.diaMes : base.diaMes,
00278:     diaSemana: typeof obj.diaSemana === "string" ? obj.diaSemana : base.diaSemana,
00279:     horario,
00280:     animal: typeof obj.animal === "string" ? obj.animal : base.animal,
00281:     posicao: typeof obj.posicao === "string" ? obj.posicao : base.posicao,
00282:   };
00283: }

00289: function isAdminHashNow() {
00290:   try {
00291:     const h = String(window.location.hash || "").trim();
00292:     return h === ADMIN_HASH || h.startsWith(`${ADMIN_HASH}?`);
00293:   } catch {
00294:     return false;
00295:   }
00296: }
00297: 
00298: async function isUidAdmin(uid) {
00299:   const u = String(uid || "").trim();
00300:   if (!u) return false;
00301:   try {
00302:     const ref = doc(db, "admins", u);
00303:     const snap = await getDoc(ref);
00304:     if (!snap.exists()) return false;
00305:     const data = snap.data() || {};
00306:     return data.active !== false;
00307:   } catch {
00308:     return false;
00309:   }
00310: }
00311: 
00312: class ErrorBoundary extends React.Component {
00313:   constructor(props) {
00314:     super(props);
00315:     this.state = { hasError: false, err: null };
00316:   }
00317: 
00318:   static getDerivedStateFromError(error) {
00319:     return { hasError: true, err: error };
00320:   }
00321: 
00322:   componentDidCatch(error, info) {
00323:     console.error("App ErrorBoundary caught:", error, info);
00324:   }
00325: 
00326:   render() {
00327:     if (this.state.hasError) {
00328:       const msg =
00329:         this.state.err?.message || String(this.state.err || "Erro desconhecido");
00330:       return (
00331:         <div
00332:           style={{
00333:             minHeight: "100vh",
00334:             background: "#050505",
00335:             color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.92)",
00336:             padding: 18,
00337:             fontFamily:
00338:               "system-ui, -apple-system, Segoe UI, Roboto, Arial, sans-serif",
00339:           }}
00340:         >
00341:           <div style={{ fontWeight: 900, marginBottom: 8 }}>
00342:             Falha ao renderizar a aplicação
00343:           </div>
00344:           <div style={{ opacity: 0.85, whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", lineHeight: 1.35 }}>
00345:             {msg}
00346:           </div>
00347:         </div>
00348:       );
00349:     }
00350: 
00351:     return this.props.children;
00352:   }
00353: }

00359: function cleanPathname(p) {
00360:   const s = String(p || "").trim();
00361:   if (!s) return "/";
00362:   return s.startsWith("/") ? s : `/${s}`;
00363: }

00365: function screenToPath(screen) {
00366:   switch (screen) {
00367:     case ROUTES.LOGIN:
00368:       return "/login";
00369:     case ROUTES.DASHBOARD:
00370:       return "/";
00371:     case ROUTES.ACCOUNT:
00372:       return "/account";
00373:     case ROUTES.RESULTS:
00374:       return "/results";
00375:     case ROUTES.TOP3:
00376:       return "/top3";
00377:     case ROUTES.LATE:
00378:       return "/late";
00379:     case ROUTES.SEARCH:
00380:       return "/search";
00381:     case ROUTES.PAYMENTS:
00382:       return "/payments";
00383:     case ROUTES.DOWNLOADS:
00384:       return "/downloads";
00385:     case ROUTES.CENTENAS:
00386:       return "/centenas";
00387:     case ROUTES.STATISTICS:
00388:       return "/statistics";
00389:     default:
00390:       return "/";
00391:   }
00392: }
00393: 
00394: function pathToScreen(pathname) {
00395:   const p = cleanPathname(pathname).toLowerCase();
00396: 
00397:   if (p === "/" || p === "/dashboard") return ROUTES.DASHBOARD;
00398:   if (p === "/login") return ROUTES.LOGIN;
00399:   if (p === "/account") return ROUTES.ACCOUNT;
00400:   if (p === "/results") return ROUTES.RESULTS;
00401:   if (p === "/top3") return ROUTES.TOP3;
00402:   if (p === "/late") return ROUTES.LATE;
00403:   if (p === "/search") return ROUTES.SEARCH;
00404:   if (p === "/payments") return ROUTES.PAYMENTS;
00405:   if (p === "/downloads") return ROUTES.DOWNLOADS;
00406:   if (p === "/centenas") return ROUTES.CENTENAS;
00407:   if (p === "/statistics") return ROUTES.STATISTICS;
00408: 
00409:   return null;
00410: }

00412: function BuildStamp() {
00413:   const shaShort = BUILD_SHA ? BUILD_SHA.slice(0, 7) : "";
00414:   const ref = BUILD_REF || "";
00415:   const tm = BUILD_TIME || "";
00416:   const text = shaShort
00417:     ? `build ${shaShort}${ref ? ` · ${ref}` : ""}${tm ? ` · ${tm}` : ""}`
00418:     : "";
00419: 
00420:   if (!shaShort) return null;
00421: 
00422:   return (
00423:     <div
00424:       style={{
00425:         position: "fixed",
00426:         left: 10,
00427:         bottom: 10,
00428:         zIndex: 99999,
00429:         padding: "7px 10px",
00430:         borderRadius: 999,
00431:         background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.62)",
00432:         border: "1px solid rgba(202,166,75,0.28)",
00433:         color: "rgba(233,233,233,0.88)",
00434:         fontSize: 11,
00435:         fontWeight: 900,
00436:         letterSpacing: 0.2,
00437:         boxShadow: "0 14px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.40)",
00438:         userSelect: "text",
00439:       }}
00440:       title={`SHA=${BUILD_SHA}${ref ? ` | ref=${ref}` : ""}${tm ? ` | time=${tm}` : ""}`}
00441:     >
00442:       {text}
00443:     </div>
00444:   );
00445: }

00460:         fontFamily:
00461:           "system-ui, -apple-system, Segoe UI, Roboto, Arial, sans-serif",
00462:       }}
00463:     >
00464:       Carregando...

00469: export default function App() {
00470:   const location = useLocation();
00471:   const navigate = useNavigate();
00472: 
00473:   const Dashboard = useMemo(() => resolveComponent(DashboardMod, "Dashboard"), []);
00474:   const Account = useMemo(() => resolveComponent(AccountMod, "Account"), []);
00475: 
00476:   const AppShell = useMemo(() => resolveComponent(AppShellMod, "AppShell"), []);
00477: 
00478: 
00479:   useEffect(() => {
00480:     console.log("[PALPITACO BUILD]", {
00481:       sha: BUILD_SHA || "(none)",
00482:       ref: BUILD_REF || "(none)",
00483:       time: BUILD_TIME || "(none)",
00484:       href: typeof window !== "undefined" ? window.location.href : "",
00485:     });
00486:   }, []);
00487: 
00488:   useEffect(() => {
00489:     cleanupLegacyGuestFlagIfNeeded();
00490:   }, []);
00491: 
00492:   const [adminMode, setAdminMode] = useState(() => isAdminHashNow());
00493: 
00494:   useEffect(() => {
00495:     const onHash = () => setAdminMode(isAdminHashNow());
00496:     window.addEventListener("hashchange", onHash);
00497:     return () => window.removeEventListener("hashchange", onHash);
00498:   }, []);
00499: 
00500:   const [adminAuthed, setAdminAuthed] = useState(false);
00501:   const [adminBooting, setAdminBooting] = useState(false);
00502: 
00503:   useEffect(() => {
00504:     if (!adminMode) return;
00505: 
00506:     let alive = true;
00507:     setAdminBooting(true);
00508:     setAdminAuthed(false);
00509: 
00510:     const unsub = onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
00511:       if (!alive) return;
00512: 
00513:       if (!user?.uid) {
00514:         setAdminAuthed(false);
00515:         setAdminBooting(false);
00516:         return;
00517:       }
00518: 
00519:       const ok = await isUidAdmin(user.uid);
00520:       if (!alive) return;
00521: 
00522:       if (!ok) {
00523:         try {
00524:           await signOut(auth);
00525:         } catch {}
00526:         setAdminAuthed(false);
00527:         setAdminBooting(false);
00528:         return;
00529:       }
00530: 
00531:       setAdminAuthed(true);
00532:       setAdminBooting(false);
00533:     });
00534: 
00535:     return () => {
00536:       alive = false;
00537:       unsub?.();
00538:     };
00539:   }, [adminMode]);
00540: 
00541:   const [dashboardFilters, setDashboardFilters] = useState(() => loadDashboardFilters());
00542:   const [, setSessionTick] = useState(0);
00543: 
00544:   useEffect(() => {
00545:     const lot = normalizeLoteriaInput(dashboardFilters?.loteria);
00546: 
00547:     if (lot === "FEDERAL") {
00548:       const h = String(dashboardFilters?.horario || "");
00549:       if (h !== "Todos" && h !== "19h" && h !== "20h") {
00550:         setDashboardFilters((prev) => ({ ...prev, horario: "Todos" }));
00551:       } else if (dashboardFilters?.loteria !== "FEDERAL") {
00552:         setDashboardFilters((prev) => ({ ...prev, loteria: "FEDERAL" }));
00553:       }
00554:       return;
00555:     }
00556: 
00557:     if (dashboardFilters?.loteria !== lot) {
00558:       setDashboardFilters((prev) => ({ ...prev, loteria: lot }));
00559:     }
00560:   }, [dashboardFilters?.loteria, dashboardFilters?.horario]);
00561: 
00562:   useEffect(() => {
00563:     safeWriteLS(DASH_FILTERS_KEY, JSON.stringify(dashboardFilters));
00564:   }, [dashboardFilters]);
00565: 
00566:   useEffect(() => {
00567:     const bump = () => setSessionTick((v) => v + 1);
00568: 
00569:     const onStorage = (e) => {
00570:       if (!e) return;
00571:       if (e.key === ACCOUNT_SESSION_KEY || e.key === LS_GUEST_ACTIVE_KEY) {
00572:         bump();
00573:       }
00574:     };
00575: 
00576:     const onSessionChanged = () => bump();
00577:     const onFocus = () => bump();
00578:     const onVis = () => {
00579:       if (document.visibilityState === "visible") bump();
00580:     };
00581: 
00582:     window.addEventListener("storage", onStorage);
00583:     window.addEventListener("pp_session_changed", onSessionChanged);
00584:     window.addEventListener("focus", onFocus);
00585:     document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
00586: 
00587:     return () => {
00588:       window.removeEventListener("storage", onStorage);
00589:       window.removeEventListener("pp_session_changed", onSessionChanged);
00590:       window.removeEventListener("focus", onFocus);
00591:       document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
00592:     };
00593:   }, []);
00594: 
00595:   const sessionObj = loadSessionObj();
00596: 
00597:   const sessionKind = useMemo(() => getSessionKind(sessionObj), [sessionObj]);
00598: 
00599:   const currentScreen = useMemo(() => {
00600:     const byPath = pathToScreen(location?.pathname);
00601:     if (byPath) return byPath;
00602: 
00603:     const saved = normalizeRoute(safeReadLS(STORAGE_KEY));
00604:     if (saved && saved !== ROUTES.LOGIN) return saved;
00605: 
00606:     return sessionObj ? ROUTES.DASHBOARD : ROUTES.LOGIN;
00607:   }, [location?.pathname, sessionObj]);
00608: 
00609:   useEffect(() => {
00610:     if (adminMode) return;
00611: 
00612:     const pathScreen = pathToScreen(location?.pathname);
00613:     const hasSession = !!sessionObj;
00614:     const curPath = cleanPathname(location?.pathname);
00615: 
00616:     if (!hasSession) {
00617:       if (curPath !== "/login") {
00618:         navigate("/login", { replace: true });
00619:       }
00620:       return;
00621:     }
00622: 
00623:     if (curPath === "/login") {
00624:       navigate("/", { replace: true });
00625:       return;
00626:     }
00627: 
00628:     if (!pathScreen) {
00629:       const saved = normalizeRoute(safeReadLS(STORAGE_KEY));
00630:       navigate(screenToPath(saved && saved !== ROUTES.LOGIN ? saved : ROUTES.DASHBOARD), {
00631:         replace: true,
00632:       });
00633:     }
00634:   }, [adminMode, location?.pathname, navigate, sessionObj]);
00635: 
00636:   useEffect(() => {
00637:     if (adminMode) return;
00638:     if (currentScreen && currentScreen !== ROUTES.LOGIN) {
00639:       safeWriteLS(STORAGE_KEY, currentScreen);
00640:     }
00641:   }, [adminMode, currentScreen]);
00642: 
00643:   const goToScreen = (nextScreen) => {
00644:     const path = screenToPath(nextScreen);
00645:     const cur = cleanPathname(location?.pathname);
00646:     if (cur !== path) {
00647:       navigate(path);
00648:     }
00649:   };
00650: 
00651:   const logout = async () => {
00652:     safeRemoveLS(STORAGE_KEY);
00653:     safeRemoveLS(ACCOUNT_SESSION_KEY);
00654:     safeRemoveLS(LS_GUEST_ACTIVE_KEY);
00655:     safeRemoveLS(DASH_FILTERS_KEY);
00656: 
00657:     try {
00658:       window.dispatchEvent(new Event("pp_session_changed"));
00659:     } catch {}
00660: 
00661:     try {
00662:       await signOut(auth);
00663:     } catch {}
00664: 
00665:     navigate("/login", { replace: true });
00666:   };
00667: 
00668:   const handleAuthenticated = () => {
00669:     cleanupLegacyGuestFlagIfNeeded();
00670:     safeWriteLS(STORAGE_KEY, ROUTES.DASHBOARD);
00671:     navigate("/", { replace: true });
00672:   };
00673: 
00674:   const renderScreen = (s) => {
00675:     switch (s) {
00676:       case ROUTES.ACCOUNT:
00677:         return <Account onAuthenticated={() => {}} />;
00678:       case ROUTES.RESULTS:
00679:         return <Results />;
00680:       case ROUTES.TOP3:
00681:         return <Top3 />;
00682:       case ROUTES.LATE:
00683:         return <Late />;
00684:       case ROUTES.SEARCH:
00685:         return <Search />;
00686:       case ROUTES.CENTENAS:
00687:         return <Centenas />;
00688:       case ROUTES.STATISTICS:
00689:         return <Statistics />;
00690:       case ROUTES.PAYMENTS:
00691:         return <Payments />;
00692:       case ROUTES.DOWNLOADS:
00693:         return <Downloads />;
00694:       default:
00695:         return (
00696:           <Dashboard
00697:             filters={{
00698:               ...dashboardFilters,
00699:               lotteryKey: loteriaToLotteryKey(dashboardFilters?.loteria),
00700:             }}
00701:             setFilters={setDashboardFilters}
00702:           />
00703:         );
00704:     }
00705:   };
00706: 
00707:   if (adminMode) {
00708:     return (
00709:       <ErrorBoundary>
00710:         <Suspense fallback={<AppLoading />}>
00711:         {adminBooting ? (
00712:           <div
00713:             style={{
00714:               minHeight: "100vh",
00715:               background: "#050505",
00716:               color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.85)",
00717:               display: "flex",
00718:               alignItems: "center",
00719:               justifyContent: "center",
00720:               fontFamily:
00721:                 "system-ui, -apple-system, Segoe UI, Roboto, Arial, sans-serif",
00722:               padding: 18,
00723:             }}
00724:           >
00725:             Carregando Admin...
00726:           </div>
00727:         ) : adminAuthed ? (
00728:           <Admin
00729:             onExit={() => {
00730:               try {
00731:                 window.location.hash = "";
00732:               } catch {}
00733:             }}
00734:             onLogout={async () => {
00735:               try {
00736:                 await signOut(auth);
00737:               } catch {}
00738:               setAdminAuthed(false);
00739:               try {
00740:                 window.location.hash = "";
00741:               } catch {}
00742:             }}
00743:           />
00744:         ) : (
00745:           <AdminLogin
00746:             onCancel={() => {
00747:               try {
00748:                 window.location.hash = "";
00749:               } catch {}
00750:             }}
00751:             onAuthed={() => {
00752:               setAdminAuthed(true);
00753:             }}
00754:           />
00755:         )}
00756:         <BuildStamp />
00757:               </Suspense>
00758:       </ErrorBoundary>
00759:     );
00760:   }
00761: 
00762:   if (sessionKind === "anon") {
00763:     return (
00764:       <ErrorBoundary>
00765:         <Suspense fallback={<AppLoading />}>
00766:         <Account
00767:           onClose={() => {}}
00768:           onAuthenticated={handleAuthenticated}
00769:         />
00770:         <BuildStamp />
00771:               </Suspense>
00772:       </ErrorBoundary>
00773:     );
00774:   }
00775: 
00776:   return (
00777:     <ErrorBoundary>
00778:         <Suspense fallback={<AppLoading />}>
00779:       <AppShell active={currentScreen} onNavigate={goToScreen} onLogout={logout}>
00780:         {currentScreen === ROUTES.DASHBOARD ? (
00781:           <Dashboard
00782:             filters={{
00783:               ...dashboardFilters,
00784:               lotteryKey: loteriaToLotteryKey(dashboardFilters?.loteria),
00785:             }}
00786:             setFilters={setDashboardFilters}
00787:           />
00788:         ) : (
00789:           renderScreen(currentScreen)
00790:         )}
00791:       </AppShell>
00792:       <BuildStamp />
00793:             </Suspense>
00794:       </ErrorBoundary>
00795:   );
00796: }

00013:     // evita poluir testes/SSR
00014:     console.warn(
00015:       `[firebase] Variável ${envKey} ausente. Usando fallback embutido para ${label}.`
00016:     );
00017:   }
00018:   return fallback;
00019: }

00066: function safeReadLS(key) {
00067:   try {
00068:     return localStorage.getItem(key);
00069:   } catch {
00070:     return null;
00071:   }
00072: }
00073:

00077:   } catch {}
00078: }

00081:   try {
00082:     localStorage.removeItem(key);
00083:   } catch {}
00084: }

00102: function resolveComponent(mod, name) {
00103:   const c = mod?.default ?? mod;
00104: 
00105:   const isProbablyReactComponent =
00106:     typeof c === "function" ||
00107:     (c && typeof c === "object" && String(c.$$typeof || "").includes("react."));
00108: 
00109:   if (!isProbablyReactComponent) {
00110:     console.error(`[IMPORT INVALID] ${name} veio inválido:`, c, " | import raw:", mod);
00111:   }
00112: 
00113:   return c;

00120: function normalizePlan(plan) {
00121:   const p = String(plan || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00122:   if (p === "VIP") return "VIP";
00123:   if (p === "PREMIUM") return "PREMIUM";
00124:   if (p === "FREE") return "FREE";
00125:   return "";
00126: }
00127: 
00128: function loadSessionObj() {
00129:   const raw = safeReadLS(ACCOUNT_SESSION_KEY);
00130:   if (!raw) return null;
00131: 
00132:   const s = String(raw || "").trim();
00133:   if (!s || !s.startsWith("{")) return null;
00134: 
00135:   const obj = safeParseJson(s);
00136:   if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return null;
00137: 
00138:   const type = String(obj.type || "").trim().toLowerCase();
00139:   const uid = String(obj.uid || "").trim();
00140:   const email = String(obj.email || "").trim().toLowerCase();
00141:   const ok = obj.ok === true;
00142:   const plan = normalizePlan(
00143:     obj.plan ??
00144:       obj.profile?.plan ??
00145:       obj.subscription?.plan ??
00146:       obj.account?.plan ??
00147:       obj.customClaims?.plan ??
00148:       obj.claims?.plan ??
00149:       obj.appData?.plan ??
00150:       obj.metadata?.plan
00151:   );
00152: 
00153:   if (!ok) return null;
00154: 
00155:   if (type === "guest") {
00156:     return {
00157:       ok: true,
00158:       type: "guest",
00159:       plan: plan || "FREE",
00160:       uid: "",
00161:       email: "",
00162:       raw: obj,
00163:     };
00164:   }
00165: 
00166:   if (type === "user" && uid) {
00167:     return {
00168:       ok: true,
00169:       type: "user",
00170:       plan: plan || "PREMIUM",
00171:       uid,
00172:       email,
00173:       raw: obj,
00174:     };
00175:   }
00176: 
00177:   if (uid || email) {
00178:     return {
00179:       ok: true,
00180:       type: "user",
00181:       plan: plan || "PREMIUM",
00182:       uid,
00183:       email,
00184:       raw: obj,
00185:     };
00186:   }
00187: 
00188:   return null;
00189: }

00191: function getSessionKind(sess) {
00192:   const s = sess || loadSessionObj();
00193:   if (!s || s.ok !== true) return "anon";
00194:   return s.type === "guest" ? "guest" : s.type === "user" ? "user" : "anon";
00195: }

00212: function normalizeLoteriaInput(v) {
00213:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim();
00214:   if (!raw) return "PT_RIO";
00215: 
00216:   const key = raw
00217:     .toLowerCase()
00218:     .normalize("NFD")
00219:     .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
00220:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
00221:     .trim();
00222: 
00223:   if (key === "federal" || key === "fed" || key === "br" || key === "brasil") {
00224:     return "FEDERAL";
00225:   }
00226: 
00227:   if (key === "rj" || key === "rio" || key === "pt_rio" || key === "pt-rio") {
00228:     return "PT_RIO";
00229:   }
00230: 
00231:   const out = key
00232:     .toUpperCase()
00233:     .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, "_")
00234:     .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
00235: 
00236:   return out || "PT_RIO";
00237: }
00238: 
00239: function loteriaToLotteryKey(loteria) {
00240:   return normalizeLoteriaInput(loteria);
00241: }
00242: 
00243: function getDefaultDashboardFilters() {
00244:   return {
00245:     loteria: "PT_RIO",
00246:     mes: "Todos",
00247:     diaMes: "Todos",
00248:     diaSemana: "Todos",
00249:     horario: "Todos",
00250:     animal: "Todos",
00251:     posicao: "Todos",
00252:   };
00253: }
00254: 
00255: function loadDashboardFilters() {
00256:   const raw = safeReadLS(DASH_FILTERS_KEY);
00257:   if (!raw) return getDefaultDashboardFilters();
00258: 
00259:   const obj = safeParseJson(raw);
00260:   if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return getDefaultDashboardFilters();
00261: 
00262:   const base = getDefaultDashboardFilters();
00263:   const loteria = normalizeLoteriaInput(obj.loteria);
00264: 
00265:   const horario =
00266:     loteria === "FEDERAL"
00267:       ? obj.horario === "Todos" || obj.horario === "19h" || obj.horario === "20h"
00268:         ? obj.horario
00269:         : "Todos"
00270:       : typeof obj.horario === "string"
00271:       ? obj.horario
00272:       : base.horario;
00273: 
00274:   return {
00275:     loteria,
00276:     mes: typeof obj.mes === "string" ? obj.mes : base.mes,
00277:     diaMes: typeof obj.diaMes === "string" ? obj.diaMes : base.diaMes,
00278:     diaSemana: typeof obj.diaSemana === "string" ? obj.diaSemana : base.diaSemana,
00279:     horario,
00280:     animal: typeof obj.animal === "string" ? obj.animal : base.animal,
00281:     posicao: typeof obj.posicao === "string" ? obj.posicao : base.posicao,
00282:   };
00283: }

00312: class ErrorBoundary extends React.Component {
00313:   constructor(props) {
00314:     super(props);
00315:     this.state = { hasError: false, err: null };
00316:   }
00317: 
00318:   static getDerivedStateFromError(error) {
00319:     return { hasError: true, err: error };
00320:   }
00321: 
00322:   componentDidCatch(error, info) {
00323:     console.error("App ErrorBoundary caught:", error, info);
00324:   }
00325: 
00326:   render() {
00327:     if (this.state.hasError) {
00328:       const msg =
00329:         this.state.err?.message || String(this.state.err || "Erro desconhecido");
00330:       return (
00331:         <div
00332:           style={{
00333:             minHeight: "100vh",
00334:             background: "#050505",
00335:             color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.92)",
00336:             padding: 18,
00337:             fontFamily:
00338:               "system-ui, -apple-system, Segoe UI, Roboto, Arial, sans-serif",
00339:           }}
00340:         >
00341:           <div style={{ fontWeight: 900, marginBottom: 8 }}>
00342:             Falha ao renderizar a aplicação
00343:           </div>
00344:           <div style={{ opacity: 0.85, whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", lineHeight: 1.35 }}>
00345:             {msg}
00346:           </div>
00347:         </div>
00348:       );
00349:     }
00350: 
00351:     return this.props.children;
00352:   }
00353: }

00412: function BuildStamp() {

00414:   const ref = BUILD_REF || "";

00417:     ? `build ${shaShort}${ref ? ` · ${ref}` : ""}${tm ? ` · ${tm}` : ""}`
00418:     : "";
00419: 
00420:   if (!shaShort) return null;
00421: 
00422:   return (
00423:     <div
00424:       style={{
00425:         position: "fixed",
00426:         left: 10,
00427:         bottom: 10,
00428:         zIndex: 99999,
00429:         padding: "7px 10px",
00430:         borderRadius: 999,
00431:         background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.62)",
00432:         border: "1px solid rgba(202,166,75,0.28)",
00433:         color: "rgba(233,233,233,0.88)",
00434:         fontSize: 11,
00435:         fontWeight: 900,
00436:         letterSpacing: 0.2,
00437:         boxShadow: "0 14px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.40)",
00438:         userSelect: "text",
00439:       }}
00440:       title={`SHA=${BUILD_SHA}${ref ? ` | ref=${ref}` : ""}${tm ? ` | time=${tm}` : ""}`}
00441:     >
00442:       {text}
00443:     </div>
00444:   );
00445: }

00027: function addDaysUtc(ymd, amount) {
00028:   if (!isYmd(ymd)) return "";
00029: 
00030:   const [year, month, day] = ymd.split("-").map(Number);
00031:   const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
00032: 
00033:   date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + Number(amount || 0));
00034: 
00035:   return [
00036:     date.getUTCFullYear(),
00037:     String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"),
00038:     String(date.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0"),
00039:   ].join("-");
00040: }

00015: function isIndexErrorMessage(err) {
00016:   const msg = String(err?.message || err || "").toLowerCase();
00017:   const code = String(err?.code || "").toLowerCase();
00018: 
00019:   return (
00020:     code.includes("failed-precondition") ||
00021:     msg.includes("failed_precondition") ||
00022:     msg.includes("requires an index") ||
00023:     (msg.includes("index") && msg.includes("create")) ||
00024:     msg.includes("falta indice") ||
00025:     msg.includes("falta índice") ||
00026:     msg.includes("falta índice composto") ||
00027:     msg.includes("falta indice composto") ||
00028:     msg.includes("firestore: falta índice") ||
00029:     msg.includes("firestore: falta indice")
00030:   );
00031: }
00032: 
00033: function normalizeToYMD(input) {
00034:   return normalizeToYMD_SP(input);
00035: }
00036: 
00037: function ymdToNumber(ymd) {
00038:   if (!ymd) return NaN;
00039:   const m = String(ymd).match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
00040:   if (!m) return NaN;
00041:   return Number(`${m[1]}${m[2]}${m[3]}`);
00042: }
00043: 
00044: function dayDiffInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo) {
00045:   const toUTC = (ymd) => {
00046:     const m = String(ymd).match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
00047:     if (!m) return null;
00048:     return new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])));
00049:   };
00050: 
00051:   const da = toUTC(ymdFrom);
00052:   const db = toUTC(ymdTo);
00053:   if (!da || !db) return NaN;
00054: 
00055:   const ms = db.getTime() - da.getTime();
00056:   if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return NaN;
00057: 
00058:   return Math.floor(ms / 86400000) + 1;
00059: }
00060: 
00061: function normalizePositionsFromKey(positionsInputKey) {
00062:   const key = String(positionsInputKey || "").trim();
00063:   if (!key) return null;
00064: 
00065:   const nums = key
00066:     .split(",")
00067:     .map((x) => Number(String(x).trim()))
00068:     .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n));
00069: 
00070:   if (!nums.length) return null;
00071:   return Array.from(new Set(nums)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
00072: }
00073: 
00074: function normHHMM(value) {
00075:   const s = String(value || "").trim();
00076:   if (!s) return "";
00077: 
00078:   const mh = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
00079:   if (mh) return `${String(mh[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00080: 
00081:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
00082:   if (mISO) {
00083:     const hh = String(mISO[1]).padStart(2, "0");
00084:     const mm = String(mISO[2]).padStart(2, "0");
00085:     return `${hh}:${mm}`;
00086:   }
00087: 
00088:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00089:   if (m2) return `${String(m2[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00090: 
00091:   return s;
00092: }
00093: 
00094: function toHourBucketLabel(closeHour) {
00095:   const hhmm = normHHMM(closeHour);
00096:   if (!hhmm) return null;
00097: 
00098:   const hh = hhmm.slice(0, 2);
00099:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(hh)) return null;
00100:   return `${hh}h`;
00101: }
00102: 
00103: function getDrawDateRaw(d) {
00104:   if (!d) return null;
00105:   return d.date ?? d.ymd ?? d.draw_date ?? d.close_date ?? d.data ?? d.dt ?? null;
00106: }
00107: 
00108: function getDrawHourRaw(d) {
00109:   if (!d) return "";
00110:   return String(d.close_hour ?? d.closeHour ?? d.hour ?? d.hora ?? "").trim();
00111: }
00112: 
00113: function getDrawLotteryCodeRaw(d) {
00114:   if (!d) return "";
00115:   return String(
00116:     d.lottery_code ??
00117:       d.lotteryCode ??
00118:       d.lot_code ??
00119:       d.lotCode ??
00120:       d.lot ??
00121:       d.code ??
00122:       d.lottery_id ??
00123:       d.lotteryId ??
00124:       ""
00125:   )
00126:     .trim()
00127:     .toUpperCase();
00128: }
00129: 
00130: function dedupeDrawsLogicalPreferBest(draws) {
00131:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00132:   if (!arr.length) return arr;
00133: 
00134:   const byKey = new Map();
00135:   const order = [];
00136: 
00137:   const score = (d) => {
00138:     const prizesLen = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes.length : 0;
00139:     const pc = Number.isFinite(Number(d?.prizesCount)) ? Number(d.prizesCount) : 0;
00140:     const ymd = normalizeToYMD(getDrawDateRaw(d));
00141:     const hhmm = normHHMM(getDrawHourRaw(d));
00142:     const hasLogical = !!(ymd && hhmm);
00143:     return prizesLen * 1000000 + pc * 1000 + (hasLogical ? 10 : 0);
00144:   };
00145: 
00146:   for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i += 1) {
00147:     const d = arr[i] || {};
00148:     const ymd = normalizeToYMD(getDrawDateRaw(d)) || "";
00149:     const hhmm = normHHMM(getDrawHourRaw(d)) || "";
00150:     const lotteryCode = getDrawLotteryCodeRaw(d) || "";
00151: 
00152:     const id =
00153:       (d?.drawId != null && String(d.drawId)) ||
00154:       (d?.id != null && String(d.id)) ||
00155:       "";
00156: 
00157:     const hasLogical = !!(ymd && hhmm);
00158: 
00159:     const key = hasLogical
00160:       ? lotteryCode
00161:         ? `${ymd}__${hhmm}__${lotteryCode}`
00162:         : `${ymd}__${hhmm}`
00163:       : `id__${id || `idx_${i}`}`;
00164: 
00165:     if (!byKey.has(key)) {
00166:       byKey.set(key, d);
00167:       order.push(key);
00168:       continue;
00169:     }
00170: 
00171:     const prev = byKey.get(key);
00172:     if (score(d) > score(prev)) {
00173:       byKey.set(key, d);
00174:     }
00175:   }
00176: 
00177:   return order.map((k) => byKey.get(k)).filter(Boolean);
00178: }
00179: 
00180: function normalizeDrawForCharts(d) {
00181:   const ymd = d?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(getDrawDateRaw(d));
00182:   const closeHour = normHHMM(getDrawHourRaw(d));
00183:   const prizes = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
00184: 
00185:   return {
00186:     ...d,
00187:     date: ymd || d?.date || null,
00188:     ymd: ymd || null,
00189:     close_hour: closeHour || "",
00190:     closeHour: closeHour || "",
00191:     lottery_code: getDrawLotteryCodeRaw(d) || d?.lottery_code || null,
00192:     prizes,
00193:   };
00194: }
00195: 
00196: function clampMs(n, min, max) {
00197:   const x = Number(n);
00198:   if (!Number.isFinite(x)) return min;
00199:   return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, x));
00200: }
00201: 
00202: function clampYmdToBounds(ymd, minYmd, maxYmd) {
00203:   if (!ymd) return null;
00204: 
00205:   const n = ymdToNumber(ymd);
00206:   const a = ymdToNumber(minYmd);
00207:   const b = ymdToNumber(maxYmd);
00208: 
00209:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return null;
00210: 
00211:   if (Number.isFinite(a) && n < a) return minYmd;
00212:   if (Number.isFinite(b) && n > b) return maxYmd;
00213:   return ymd;
00214: }
00215: 
00216: function clampRangeToBounds(ymdFrom, ymdTo, minYmd, maxYmd) {
00217:   let a = ymdFrom || null;
00218:   let b = ymdTo || null;
00219: 
00220:   if (minYmd || maxYmd) {
00221:     a = clampYmdToBounds(a, minYmd, maxYmd);
00222:     b = clampYmdToBounds(b, minYmd, maxYmd);
00223:   }
00224: 
00225:   const na = ymdToNumber(a);
00226:   const nb = ymdToNumber(b);
00227:   if (Number.isFinite(na) && Number.isFinite(nb) && na > nb) {
00228:     return { ymdFrom: b, ymdTo: a };
00229:   }
00230: 
00231:   return { ymdFrom: a, ymdTo: b };
00232: }
00233: 
00234: function normalizeBucketInput(bucket) {
00235:   const s = String(bucket || "").trim();
00236:   if (!s) return null;
00237:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
00238:   if (m) return `${String(m[1]).padStart(2, "0")}h`;
00239:   return s;
00240: }

00243: const BOUNDS_TTL_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;
00244: 
00245: function nowMs() {
00246:   return Date.now();
00247: }

00249: const INITIAL_BOUNDS = {
00250:   ok: false,
00251:   uf: null,
00252:   minYmd: null,
00253:   maxYmd: null,
00254:   source: "none",
00255: };

00258:   top3: [],
00259:   totalOcorrencias: 0,
00260:   totalDraws: 0,
00261:   mode: "none",
00262:   date: null,
00263:   dateFrom: null,
00264:   dateTo: null,
00265: 
00266:   palpitesByGrupo: {},
00267:   palpiteSampleDrawsUsed: 0,
00268:   palpiteUsedBucket: null,
00269: 
00270:   bounds: { ...INITIAL_BOUNDS },
00271:   suggestedRange: { from: null, to: null },
00272: 
00273:   serviceMode: "detailed",
00274:   hydrating: false,
00275: };

00277: export function useKingRanking({
00278:   uf,
00279:   date,
00280:   dateFrom,
00281:   dateTo,
00282:   closeHourBucket = null,
00283:   positions = null,
00284:   needsPrizes = false,
00285: }) {
00286:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
00287:   const [error, setError] = useState(null);
00288: 
00289:   const [data, setData] = useState([]);
00290:   const [meta, setMeta] = useState(INITIAL_META);
00291: 
00292:   const [drawsRaw, setDrawsRaw] = useState([]);
00293: 
00294:   const boundsCacheRef = useRef(new Map());
00295:   const [bounds, setBounds] = useState(INITIAL_BOUNDS);
00296: 
00297:   const [boundsRetryTick, setBoundsRetryTick] = useState(0);
00298:   const boundsRetryTimerRef = useRef(null);
00299:   const [boundsSoftTick, setBoundsSoftTick] = useState(0);
00300: 
00301:   useEffect(() => {
00302:     const onFocus = () => setBoundsSoftTick((t) => t + 1);
00303:     if (typeof window !== "undefined") window.addEventListener("focus", onFocus);
00304:     return () => {
00305:       if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
00306:         window.removeEventListener("focus", onFocus);
00307:       }
00308:     };
00309:   }, []);
00310: 
00311:   useEffect(() => {
00312:     let mounted = true;
00313: 
00314:     async function loadBounds() {
00315:       const key = String(uf || "").trim();
00316:       if (!key) {
00317:         if (mounted) setBounds(INITIAL_BOUNDS);
00318:         return;
00319:       }
00320: 
00321:       if (boundsCacheRef.current.has(key)) {
00322:         const cachedWrap = boundsCacheRef.current.get(key);
00323:         const age = nowMs() - Number(cachedWrap?.ts || 0);
00324: 
00325:         if (cachedWrap?.data && Number.isFinite(age) && age < BOUNDS_TTL_MS) {
00326:           if (mounted) setBounds(cachedWrap.data);
00327:           return;
00328:         }
00329:       }
00330: 
00331:       try {
00332:         const b = await getKingBoundsByUf({ uf: key });
00333:         const safe = {
00334:           ok: !!b?.ok,
00335:           uf: key,
00336:           minYmd: b?.minYmd || null,
00337:           maxYmd: b?.maxYmd || null,
00338:           source: b?.source || "none",
00339:         };
00340: 
00341:         boundsCacheRef.current.set(key, { ts: nowMs(), data: safe });
00342: 
00343:         if (mounted) setBounds(safe);
00344: 
00345:         if (mounted && !safe.ok) {
00346:           if (boundsRetryTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(boundsRetryTimerRef.current);
00347:           boundsRetryTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
00348:             if (mounted) setBoundsRetryTick((t) => t + 1);
00349:           }, 10000);
00350:         }
00351:       } catch {
00352:         const safe = {
00353:           ok: false,
00354:           uf: key,
00355:           minYmd: null,
00356:           maxYmd: null,
00357:           source: "none",
00358:         };
00359: 
00360:         boundsCacheRef.current.set(key, { ts: nowMs(), data: safe });
00361: 
00362:         if (mounted) setBounds(safe);
00363: 
00364:         if (boundsRetryTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(boundsRetryTimerRef.current);
00365:         boundsRetryTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
00366:           if (mounted) setBoundsRetryTick((t) => t + 1);
00367:         }, 10000);
00368:       }
00369:     }
00370: 
00371:     loadBounds();
00372: 
00373:     return () => {
00374:       mounted = false;
00375:       if (boundsRetryTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(boundsRetryTimerRef.current);
00376:     };
00377:   }, [uf, boundsRetryTick, boundsSoftTick]);
00378: 
00379:   const ymdDateRaw = useMemo(() => normalizeToYMD(date), [date]);
00380:   const ymdFromRaw = useMemo(() => normalizeToYMD(dateFrom), [dateFrom]);
00381:   const ymdToRaw = useMemo(() => normalizeToYMD(dateTo), [dateTo]);
00382: 
00383:   const bucketNorm = useMemo(() => normalizeBucketInput(closeHourBucket), [closeHourBucket]);
00384: 
00385:   const positionsEffective = useMemo(() => {
00386:     return Array.isArray(positions) && positions.length ? positions : null;
00387:   }, [positions]);
00388: 
00389:   const positionsInputKey = useMemo(() => {
00390:     if (!Array.isArray(positionsEffective) || !positionsEffective.length) return "";
00391:     return positionsEffective.map((n) => String(n)).join(",");
00392:   }, [positionsEffective]);
00393: 
00394:   const positionsArrStable = useMemo(
00395:     () => normalizePositionsFromKey(positionsInputKey),
00396:     [positionsInputKey]
00397:   );
00398: 
00399:   const positionsKey = useMemo(
00400:     () => (positionsArrStable ? positionsArrStable.join(",") : ""),
00401:     [positionsArrStable]
00402:   );
00403: 
00404:   const hourBucketKey = useMemo(() => String(bucketNorm || ""), [bucketNorm]);
00405: 
00406:   const ymdDate = useMemo(() => {
00407:     return clampYmdToBounds(ymdDateRaw, bounds.minYmd, bounds.maxYmd);
00408:   }, [ymdDateRaw, bounds.minYmd, bounds.maxYmd]);
00409: 
00410:   const { ymdFrom, ymdTo } = useMemo(() => {
00411:     const na = ymdToNumber(ymdFromRaw);
00412:     const nb = ymdToNumber(ymdToRaw);
00413: 
00414:     const a0 =
00415:       Number.isFinite(na) && Number.isFinite(nb) && na > nb ? ymdToRaw : ymdFromRaw;
00416:     const b0 =
00417:       Number.isFinite(na) && Number.isFinite(nb) && na > nb ? ymdFromRaw : ymdToRaw;
00418: 
00419:     return clampRangeToBounds(a0, b0, bounds.minYmd, bounds.maxYmd);
00420:   }, [ymdFromRaw, ymdToRaw, bounds.minYmd, bounds.maxYmd]);
00421: 
00422:   const effectiveDayYmd = useMemo(() => {
00423:     if (ymdDate) return ymdDate;

00425:     return null;
00426:   }, [uf, ymdDate, ymdFrom, ymdTo]);
00427: 
00428:   const mode = useMemo(() => {
00429:     if (uf && ymdFrom && ymdTo && ymdFrom !== ymdTo) return "range";
00430:     if (uf && effectiveDayYmd) return "day";
00431:     return "none";
00432:   }, [uf, ymdFrom, ymdTo, effectiveDayYmd]);
00433: 
00434:   const rangeDays = useMemo(() => {
00435:     if (mode !== "range") return NaN;
00436:     return dayDiffInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo);
00437:   }, [mode, ymdFrom, ymdTo]);
00438: 
00439:   const RANGE_FALLBACK_LIMIT_DAYS = 31;
00440: 
00441:   const [rangeBlocked, setRangeBlocked] = useState(false);
00442:   const rangeBlockedKeyRef = useRef("");
00443: 
00444:   useEffect(() => {
00445:     const k = [mode, uf || "", ymdFrom || "", ymdTo || ""].join("|");
00446:     if (rangeBlockedKeyRef.current !== k) {
00447:       rangeBlockedKeyRef.current = k;
00448:       setRangeBlocked(false);
00449:     }
00450:   }, [mode, uf, ymdFrom, ymdTo]);
00451: 
00452:   const autoRefreshEnabled = useMemo(() => {
00453:     if (mode === "none") return false;
00454: 
00455:     if (
00456:       mode === "range" &&
00457:       rangeBlocked &&
00458:       Number.isFinite(rangeDays) &&
00459:       rangeDays > RANGE_FALLBACK_LIMIT_DAYS
00460:     ) {
00461:       return false;
00462:     }
00463: 
00464:     if (mode === "day") return true;
00465: 
00466:     if (!Number.isFinite(rangeDays)) return true;
00467: 
00468:     const REFRESH_MAX_DAYS = 45;
00469:     return rangeDays <= REFRESH_MAX_DAYS;
00470:   }, [mode, rangeDays, rangeBlocked]);
00471: 
00472:   const AUTO_REFRESH_MS = 60000;
00473:   const intervalMs = useMemo(() => clampMs(AUTO_REFRESH_MS, 30000, 300000), []);
00474: 
00475:   const [refreshTick, setRefreshTick] = useState(0);
00476:   const refreshSeqRef = useRef(0);
00477: 
00478:   const boundsKey = useMemo(
00479:     () => `${bounds.minYmd || ""}|${bounds.maxYmd || ""}`,
00480:     [bounds.minYmd, bounds.maxYmd]
00481:   );
00482:

00484:     () => ({
00485:       ok: !!bounds?.ok,
00486:       uf: bounds?.uf || uf || null,
00487:       minYmd: bounds?.minYmd || null,
00488:       maxYmd: bounds?.maxYmd || null,
00489:       source: bounds?.source || "none",
00490:     }),

00492:   );
00493: 
00494:   const hardKey = useMemo(() => {
00495:     return [
00496:       mode,
00497:       uf || "",
00498:       effectiveDayYmd || "",
00499:       ymdFrom || "",
00500:       ymdTo || "",
00501:       hourBucketKey || "",
00502:       positionsKey || "",
00503:       boundsKey,
00504:     ].join("|");

00506: 
00507:   const lastHardKeyRef = useRef("");
00508: 
00509:   useEffect(() => {
00510:     if (!autoRefreshEnabled) return;
00511: 
00512:     const id = setInterval(() => {
00513:       if (typeof document !== "undefined" && document.hidden) return;
00514:       setRefreshTick((t) => t + 1);
00515:     }, intervalMs);
00516: 
00517:     return () => clearInterval(id);
00518:   }, [intervalMs, autoRefreshEnabled]);
00519: 
00520:   useEffect(() => {
00521:     const bump = () => setRefreshTick((t) => t + 1);
00522: 
00523:     const onVisibility = () => {
00524:       if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
00525:       if (!document.hidden) bump();
00526:     };
00527:

00540: 
00541:   useEffect(() => {

00600:         }

00602:         setDrawsRaw(unique);
00603:

00605:

00607:           if (hasAnyPrize) {

00612:               minDraws: 400,

00617:               ...prev,
00618:               top3: built.top3 || [],

00632:                 from: boundsSnapshot?.minYmd || null,
00633:                 to: boundsSnapshot?.maxYmd || null,
00634:               },
00635: 
00636:               serviceMode,
00637:               hydrating: false,
00638:             }));
00639:           } else {
00640:             setData([]);
00641:             setMeta((prev) => ({
00642:               ...prev,
00643:               top3: [],
00644:               totalOcorrencias: 0,
00645:               totalDraws: unique.length,
00646:               mode,
00647:               date: mode === "day" ? effectiveDayYmd : null,
00648:               dateFrom: mode === "range" ? ymdFrom : null,
00649:               dateTo: mode === "range" ? ymdTo : null,
00650:

00660:

00679: 
00680:           palpitesByGrupo: {},
00681:           palpiteSampleDrawsUsed: 0,

00683:

00688:           },
00689: 
00690:           serviceMode: "aggregated",
00691:           hydrating: false,
00692:         }));
00693:       } catch (e) {
00694:         if (!mounted) return;
00695:         if (refreshSeqRef.current && mySeq !== refreshSeqRef.current) return;
00696: 
00697:         if (
00698:           mode === "range" &&
00699:           isIndexErrorMessage(e) &&
00700:           Number.isFinite(rangeDays) &&
00701:           rangeDays > RANGE_FALLBACK_LIMIT_DAYS
00702:         ) {
00703:           setRangeBlocked(true);
00704:         }
00705: 
00706:         if (hard) {
00707:           setDrawsRaw([]);
00708:           setData([]);
00709:         }
00710: 
00711:         setMeta((prev) => ({
00712:           ...prev,
00713:           hydrating: false,
00714:         }));
00715: 
00716:         setError(e instanceof Error ? e : new Error("Erro desconhecido"));
00717:       } finally {
00718:         if (mounted && mySeq === refreshSeqRef.current) {
00719:           setLoading(false);
00720:         }
00721:       }
00722:     }
00723: 
00724:     if (mode === "none") {
00725:       setError(null);
00726:       setDrawsRaw([]);
00727:       setData([]);
00728:       setMeta(INITIAL_META);
00729:       setLoading(false);
00730:       return () => {
00731:         mounted = false;
00732:       };
00733:     }
00734: 
00735:     const isHard = lastHardKeyRef.current !== hardKey;
00736:     lastHardKeyRef.current = hardKey;
00737: 
00738:     load({ hard: isHard });
00739: 
00740:     return () => {
00741:       mounted = false;
00742:     };
00743:   }, [
00744:     mode,
00745:     uf,
00746:     effectiveDayYmd,
00747:     ymdFrom,
00748:     ymdTo,
00749:     hourBucketKey,
00750:     positionsKey,
00751:     positionsArrStable,
00752:     hardKey,
00753:     refreshTick,
00754:     bucketNorm,
00755:     boundsKey,
00756:     rangeDays,
00757:     needsPrizes,
00758:     rangeBlocked,
00759:     boundsSnapshot,
00760:   ]);
00761: 
00762:   return { loading, error, data, meta, drawsRaw };
00763: }

00025:   try {
00026:     return localStorage.getItem(k);
00027:   } catch {
00028:     return null;
00029:   }
00030: };

00034:     localStorage.removeItem(k);

00038: function normalizePlan(plan) {
00039:   const p = String(plan || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00040:   if (p === "VIP") return "VIP";
00041:   if (p === "PREMIUM") return "PREMIUM";
00042:   if (p === "FREE") return "FREE";
00043:   return "";
00044: }
00045: 
00046: function readSession() {
00047:   const raw = safeReadLS(ACCOUNT_SESSION_KEY);
00048:   if (!raw) {
00049:     return {
00050:       ok: false,
00051:       type: "anon",
00052:       plan: "",
00053:       uid: "",
00054:       email: "",
00055:       raw: null,
00056:     };
00057:   }
00058: 
00059:   try {
00060:     const obj = JSON.parse(raw);
00061:     const type = String(obj?.type || "").trim().toLowerCase();
00062:     const plan = normalizePlan(obj?.plan);
00063: 
00064:     return {
00065:       ok: obj?.ok === true,
00066:       type: type || "anon",
00067:       plan: type === "guest" ? "FREE" : plan || (type === "user" ? "FREE" : ""),
00068:       uid: String(obj?.uid || "").trim(),
00069:       email: String(obj?.email || "").trim().toLowerCase(),
00070:       raw: obj,
00071:     };
00072:   } catch {
00073:     return {
00074:       ok: true,
00075:       type: "user",
00076:       plan: "FREE",
00077:       uid: "",
00078:       email: "",
00079:       raw: null,
00080:     };
00081:   }
00082: }

00084: function useViewport() {
00085:   const [vw, setVw] = useState(() =>
00086:     typeof window !== "undefined" ? window.innerWidth : 1200
00087:   );
00088: 
00089:   useEffect(() => {
00090:     const onR = () => setVw(window.innerWidth);
00091:     window.addEventListener("resize", onR);
00092:     return () => window.removeEventListener("resize", onR);
00093:   }, []);
00094: 
00095:   return vw;
00096: }

00182:       document.body.style.overflow = prevBodyOverflow || "";
00183:     }
00184:

00186:       document.documentElement.style.overflow = prevHtmlOverflow || "";
00187:       document.body.style.overflow = prevBodyOverflow || "";
00188:     };

00057: function safeParseJSON(s) {
00058:   try {
00059:     const obj = JSON.parse(s);
00060:     return obj && typeof obj === "object" ? obj : null;
00061:   } catch {
00062:     return null;
00063:   }
00064: }
00065: 
00066: function loadSessionObj() {
00067:   try {
00068:     const raw = localStorage.getItem(ACCOUNT_SESSION_KEY);
00069:     if (!raw) return null;
00070:     const s = String(raw || "").trim();
00071:     if (!s.startsWith("{")) return null;
00072:     return safeParseJSON(s);
00073:   } catch {
00074:     return null;
00075:   }
00076: }
00077: 
00078: function normalizeSessionPlan(plan) {
00079:   const p = String(plan || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00080:   if (p === "VIP") return "VIP";
00081:   if (p === "PREMIUM") return "PREMIUM";
00082:   if (p === "FREE") return "FREE";
00083:   return "";
00084: }
00085: 
00086: function getSessionKind(sess) {
00087:   const s = sess || loadSessionObj();
00088:   if (!s || s.ok !== true) return "anon";
00089: 
00090:   const type = String(s.type || "").trim().toLowerCase();
00091:   if (type === "guest") return "guest";
00092:   if (type === "user") return "user";
00093: 
00094:   const loginType = String(s.loginType || "").trim().toLowerCase();
00095:   const loginId = String(s.loginId || "").trim().toLowerCase();
00096:   const mode = String(s.mode || "").trim().toLowerCase();
00097: 
00098:   if (
00099:     loginType === "guest" ||
00100:     loginId === "guest" ||
00101:     s.skipped === true ||
00102:     mode === "skip"
00103:   ) {
00104:     return "guest";
00105:   }
00106: 
00107:   if (loginType === "user" || loginId === "user") {
00108:     return "user";
00109:   }
00110: 
00111:   if (s.uid || s.email) return "user";
00112: 
00113:   return "anon";
00114: }
00115: 
00116: function getSessionPlan(sess) {
00117:   const s = sess || loadSessionObj();
00118:   if (!s || s.ok !== true) return "";
00119: 
00120:   const normalized = normalizeSessionPlan(
00121:     s.plan ??
00122:       s.profile?.plan ??
00123:       s.subscription?.plan ??
00124:       s.account?.plan ??
00125:       s.customClaims?.plan ??
00126:       s.claims?.plan ??
00127:       s.appData?.plan ??
00128:       s.metadata?.plan
00129:   );
00130: 
00131:   if (normalized) return normalized;
00132: 
00133:   const kind = getSessionKind(s);
00134:   if (kind === "guest") return "FREE";
00135:   if (kind === "user") return "PREMIUM";
00136: 
00137:   return "";
00138: }

00150:   } catch {

00158:   } catch {
00159:     // ignore
00160:   }

00163: function isValidGrupo(g) {
00164:   const n = Number(g);
00165:   return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 25;
00166: }
00167: 
00168: function preloadImage(src) {
00169:   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
00170:     if (!src) return reject(new Error("src vazio"));
00171:     const img = new Image();
00172:     img.onload = () => resolve(true);
00173:     img.onerror = () => reject(new Error("falha ao carregar"));
00174:     img.src = src;
00175:   });
00176: }

00182: function normalizeHourBucket(h) {
00183:   const raw = String(h ?? "").trim();
00184:   if (!raw || raw.toLowerCase() === "todos") return null;
00185: 
00186:   const s = raw.replace(/\s+/g, "").toUpperCase();
00187: 
00188:   let m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:H|HS)$/);
00189:   if (m) return `${String(m[1]).padStart(2, "0")}h`;
00190: 
00191:   m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::\d{2})?$/);
00192:   if (m) return `${String(m[1]).padStart(2, "0")}h`;
00193: 
00194:   m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00195:   if (m) return `${String(m[1]).padStart(2, "0")}h`;
00196: 
00197:   m = s.match(/(\d{1,2})/);
00198:   if (m) {
00199:     const hh = Number(m[1]);
00200:     if (Number.isFinite(hh) && hh >= 0 && hh <= 23) return `${String(hh).padStart(2, "0")}h`;
00201:   }
00202: 
00203:   return null;
00204: }
00205: 
00206: function normalizePositions(pos) {
00207:   const s = String(pos || "").trim();
00208:   if (!s || s.toLowerCase() === "todos") return [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7];
00209: 
00210:   const m = s.match(/^(\d+)/);
00211:   if (m) {
00212:     const n = Number(m[1]);
00213:     if (Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 7) return [n];
00214:   }
00215: 
00216:   return [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7];
00217: }
00218: 
00219: function pad2(n) {
00220:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
00221: }
00222: 
00223: function isISODate(s) {
00224:   return /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.test(String(s || ""));
00225: }
00226: 
00227: function normalizeISO10(v) {
00228:   const s = String(v || "").trim();
00229:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/);
00230:   const iso = m ? m[1] : "";
00231:   return isISODate(iso) ? iso : null;
00232: }
00233: 
00234: function normalizeToYMD(input) {
00235:   return normalizeToYMD_SP(input);
00236: }
00237: 
00238: function getDrawDate(d) {
00239:   if (!d) return null;
00240:   const raw = d.date ?? d.ymd ?? d.draw_date ?? d.close_date ?? d.data ?? d.dt ?? null;
00241:   return normalizeToYMD(raw);
00242: }

00244: function getDrawCloseHour(d) {
00245:   if (!d) return "";
00246: 
00247:   const cand =
00248:     d.close_hour_raw ??
00249:     d.closeHourRaw ??
00250:     d.close_hour ??

00466:   return (
00467:     msg.includes("failed-precondition") ||

00476: function clampRangeToBounds(next, minDate, maxDate) {
00477:   const f = next?.from;
00478:   const t = next?.to;
00479: 
00480:   if (!isISODate(f) || !isISODate(t)) return null;
00481:   if (!minDate || !maxDate) return null;
00482:   if (!isISODate(minDate) || !isISODate(maxDate)) return null;
00483: 
00484:   let from = f < minDate ? minDate : f;
00485:   let to = t > maxDate ? maxDate : t;
00486: 
00487:   if (from > to) to = from;
00488:   return { from, to };
00489: }

00491: function normalizeLoteriaKey(v) {
00492:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim();
00493: 
00494:   if (!raw) return "PT_RIO";
00495: 
00496:   const key = raw
00497:     .toLowerCase()
00498:     .normalize("NFD")
00499:     .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
00500:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
00501:     .trim();
00502: 
00503:   if (key === "federal" || key === "fed" || key === "br" || key === "brasil") return "FEDERAL";
00504:   if (key === "rj" || key === "rio" || key === "pt_rio" || key === "pt-rio") return "PT_RIO";
00505: 
00506:   const out = key
00507:     .toUpperCase()
00508:     .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, "_")
00509:     .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
00510: 
00511:   return out || "PT_RIO";
00512: }

00514: function getDefaultFloorByUf(ufKey) {

00522:     return "2022-06-08";

00524:

00530:     return "2022-06-07";
00531:   }
00532: 
00533:   return "2022-01-01";

00536: function getTodayYmdSafe() {

00547: function yearRangeToDates(minY, maxY, floorISO, ceilISO) {
00548:   const y1 = Number(minY);
00549:   const y2 = Number(maxY);
00550:   if (!Number.isFinite(y1) || !Number.isFinite(y2)) return null;
00551: 
00552:   const a = Math.min(y1, y2);
00553:   const b = Math.max(y1, y2);
00554: 
00555:   let from = `${a}-01-01`;
00556:   let to = `${b}-12-31`;
00557: 
00558:   if (isISODate(floorISO) && from < floorISO) from = floorISO;
00559:   if (isISODate(ceilISO) && to > ceilISO) to = ceilISO;
00560: 
00561:   if (isISODate(from) && isISODate(to) && from > to) to = from;
00562:   return { from, to };
00563: }

00565: function normalizeCloseHourForKpi(raw) {

00576:   if (m) return `${String(m[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00577: 
00578:   m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})/);
00579:   if (m) return `${String(m[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00580: 
00581:   return "";
00582: }

00617: function dedupeDrawsForKpi(list, ufKey) {
00618:   const arr = Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];
00619:   if (!arr.length) return [];
00620:   const seen = new Set();
00621:   const out = [];
00622: 
00623:   for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i += 1) {
00624:     const d = arr[i];
00625:     const k = getDrawUniqKeyForKpi(d, ufKey, i);
00626:     if (!k) continue;
00627:     if (seen.has(k)) continue;
00628:     seen.add(k);
00629:     out.push(d);
00630:   }
00631: 
00632:   return out;
00633: }

00635: function buildYearsAvailable(minISO, maxISO) {
00636:   if (!isISODate(minISO) || !isISODate(maxISO)) return [];

00638:   const y2 = Number(String(maxISO).slice(0, 4));
00639:   if (!Number.isFinite(y1) || !Number.isFinite(y2)) return [];
00640:   const a = Math.min(y1, y2);
00641:   const b = Math.max(y1, y2);
00642:   const out = [];
00643:   for (let y = a; y <= b; y++) out.push(y);
00644:   return out;
00645: }

00687: export default function Dashboard(props) {
00688:   const externalFilters = props && typeof props === "object" ? props.filters : null;
00689:   const externalSetFilters = props && typeof props === "object" ? props.setFilters : null;
00690: 
00691:   const fallbackFilters = useMemo(
00692:     () => ({
00693:       loteria: "PT_RIO",
00694:       mes: "Todos",
00695:       diaMes: "Todos",
00696:       diaSemana: "Todos",
00697:       horario: "Todos",
00698:       animal: "Todos",
00699:       posicao: "Todos",
00700:     }),
00701:     []
00702:   );
00703: 
00704:   const [, setSessionObj] = useState(() => loadSessionObj());
00705:   const [sessionKind, setSessionKind] = useState(() => getSessionKind(loadSessionObj()));
00706:   const [, setSessionPlan] = useState(() => getSessionPlan(loadSessionObj()));
00707: 
00708:   const isGuest = sessionKind === "guest";
00709: 
00710:   useEffect(() => {
00711:     const serializeSession = (sess) => {
00712:       try {
00713:         return JSON.stringify(sess || null);
00714:       } catch {
00715:         return "__SERIALIZE_ERROR__";
00716:       }
00717:     };
00718: 
00719:     let lastSig = serializeSession(loadSessionObj());
00720: 
00721:     const refresh = () => {
00722:       const sess = loadSessionObj();
00723:       const nextSig = serializeSession(sess);
00724: 
00725:       if (nextSig === lastSig) return;
00726: 
00727:       lastSig = nextSig;
00728:       setSessionObj(sess);
00729:       setSessionKind(getSessionKind(sess));
00730:       setSessionPlan(getSessionPlan(sess));
00731:     };
00732: 
00733:     const onStorage = (e) => {
00734:       if (e && e.key && e.key !== ACCOUNT_SESSION_KEY) return;
00735:       refresh();
00736:     };
00737: 
00738:     const onVisibility = () => {
00739:       if (document.visibilityState === "visible") refresh();
00740:     };
00741: 
00742:     const onSessionChanged = () => refresh();
00743: 
00744:     window.addEventListener("storage", onStorage);
00745:     window.addEventListener("pp_session_changed", onSessionChanged);
00746:     document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
00747: 
00748:     const t = setInterval(() => {
00749:       if (document.visibilityState !== "visible") return;
00750:       refresh();
00751:     }, SESSION_POLL_MS);
00752: 
00753:     return () => {
00754:       window.removeEventListener("storage", onStorage);
00755:       window.removeEventListener("pp_session_changed", onSessionChanged);
00756:       document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
00757:       clearInterval(t);
00758:     };
00759:   }, []);
00760: 
00761:   const rawFilters = useMemo(() => {
00762:     return externalFilters && typeof externalFilters === "object" ? externalFilters : fallbackFilters;
00763:   }, [externalFilters, fallbackFilters]);
00764: 
00765:   const filters = useMemo(() => {
00766:     return { ...fallbackFilters, ...rawFilters };
00767:   }, [rawFilters, fallbackFilters]);
00768: 
00769:   const noopSetFilters = useCallback(() => {}, []);
00770:   const setFilters = useMemo(() => {
00771:     return typeof externalSetFilters === "function" ? externalSetFilters : noopSetFilters;
00772:   }, [externalSetFilters, noopSetFilters]);
00773: 
00774:   const loteriaKey = useMemo(
00775:     () => normalizeLoteriaKey(filters?.lotteryKey || filters?.loteria || filters?.uf),
00776:     [filters?.lotteryKey, filters?.loteria, filters?.uf]
00777:   );
00778: 
00779:   const isFederal = loteriaKey === "FEDERAL";
00780: 
00781:   const fedBucket = isFederal ? normalizeHourBucket(filters?.horario) : null;
00782:   const locationLabel = isFederal
00783:     ? `FEDERAL (Brasil)${fedBucket ? ` — ${fedBucket}` : ""}`
00784:     : `${loteriaKey} — Brasil`;
00785: 
00786:   const uf = loteriaKey;
00787: 
00788:   const [savedDashState, setSavedDashState] = useState(() => loadDashStateV2(uf));
00789: 
00790:   useEffect(() => {
00791:     setSavedDashState(loadDashStateV2(uf));
00792:   }, [uf]);
00793: 
00794:   const [selectedGrupo, setSelectedGrupo] = useState(() => {
00795:     const g = Number(savedDashState?.selectedGrupo);
00796:     return Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25 ? g : null;
00797:   });
00798: 
00799:   const didInitRangeFromBoundsRef = useRef(false);
00800:   const debounceTimerRef = useRef(null);
00801: 
00802:   const [followMax, setFollowMax] = useState(() => savedDashState?.followMax !== false);
00803: 
00804:   const [dateRange, setDateRange] = useState(() => {
00805:     const dr = savedDashState?.dateRange;
00806:     if (dr?.from && dr?.to && isISODate(dr.from) && isISODate(dr.to)) {
00807:       return { from: dr.from, to: dr.to };
00808:     }
00809:     return null;
00810:   });
00811: 
00812:   const [dateRangeQuery, setDateRangeQuery] = useState(() => {
00813:     const dr = savedDashState?.dateRangeQuery;
00814:     if (dr?.from && dr?.to && isISODate(dr.from) && isISODate(dr.to)) {
00815:       return { from: dr.from, to: dr.to };
00816:     }
00817:     return null;
00818:   });
00819: 
00820:   const [selectedYears, setSelectedYears] = useState(() => {
00821:     const arr = Array.isArray(savedDashState?.selectedYears) ? savedDashState.selectedYears : [];
00822:     return arr.map((y) => Number(y)).filter((y) => Number.isFinite(y));
00823:   });
00824:

00826:     loading: DATA_MODE === "firestore",

00828:     maxDate: null,
00829:     error: null,
00830:   });
00831: 
00832:   useEffect(() => {
00833:     if (isGuest) return;
00834: 
00835:     const st = loadDashStateV2(uf) || null;
00836: 
00837:     didInitRangeFromBoundsRef.current = false;
00838: 
00839:     setSelectedGrupo(() => {

00001: /* eslint-disable no-unused-vars */
00002: // src/pages/Results/Results.jsx
00003: import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
00004: import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
00005: import {
00006:   getKingBoundsByUf,
00007:   getKingResultsByDate,
00008:   getKingResultsByRange,
00009: } from "../../services/kingResultsService";

00011:   getAnimalLabel,
