00016: /* =========================
00017:    Helpers (locais e robustos)
00018: ========================= */
00019: 
00020: function pad2(n) {
00021:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
00022: }
00023: 
00024: function safeStr(v) {
00025:   return String(v ?? "").trim();
00026: }
00027: 
00028: function isYMD(s) {
00029:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
00030: }
00031: 
00032: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
00033:   const m = String(ymd || "")
00034:     .trim()
00035:     .match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
00036:   if (!m) return safeStr(ymd);
00037:   return `${m[3]}/${m[2]}/${m[1]}`;
00038: }
00039: 
00040: function nowLocal() {
00041:   return new Date();
00042: }
00043: 
00044: function todayYMDLocal() {
00045:   const d = nowLocal();
00046:   return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
00047: }
00048: 
00049: function currentHourNumLocal() {
00050:   const d = nowLocal();
00051:   return d.getHours() * 100 + d.getMinutes();
00052: }
00053: 
00054: function ymdToDateLocal(ymd) {
00055:   const m = String(ymd || "")
00056:     .trim()
00057:     .match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
00058:   if (!m) return null;
00059:   return new Date(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3]), 12, 0, 0);
00060: }
00061: 
00062: function dateToYMDLocal(d) {
00063:   if (!(d instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return "";
00064:   return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
00065: }
00066: 
00067: function startOfMonthYMD(ymd) {
00068:   const d = ymdToDateLocal(ymd);
00069:   if (!d) return todayYMDLocal().slice(0, 8) + "01";
00070:   d.setDate(1);
00071:   return dateToYMDLocal(d);
00072: }
00073: 
00074: function endOfMonthYMD(ymd) {
00075:   const d = ymdToDateLocal(ymd);
00076:   if (!d) return todayYMDLocal();
00077:   d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + 1, 0);
00078:   return dateToYMDLocal(d);
00079: }
00080: 
00081: function shiftMonthYMD(ymd, deltaMonths) {
00082:   const d = ymdToDateLocal(startOfMonthYMD(ymd));
00083:   if (!d) return startOfMonthYMD(todayYMDLocal());
00084:   d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + Number(deltaMonths || 0), 1);
00085:   return dateToYMDLocal(d);
00086: }
00087: 
00088: function monthTitleBR(ymd) {
00089:   const d = ymdToDateLocal(startOfMonthYMD(ymd));
00090:   if (!d) return "";
00091:   const s = d.toLocaleDateString("pt-BR", { month: "long", year: "numeric" });
00092:   return s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);
00093: }
00094: 
00095: function buildCalendarCells(monthYmd) {
00096:   const first = ymdToDateLocal(startOfMonthYMD(monthYmd));
00097:   if (!first) return [];
00098: 
00099:   const start = new Date(first);
00100:   start.setDate(first.getDate() - first.getDay());
00101: 
00102:   return Array.from({ length: 42 }, (_, idx) => {
00103:     const d = new Date(start);
00104:     d.setDate(start.getDate() + idx);
00105:     const ymd = dateToYMDLocal(d);
00106:     return {
00107:       ymd,
00108:       day: d.getDate(),
00109:       inMonth: d.getMonth() === first.getMonth(),
00110:     };
00111:   });
00112: }
00113: 
00114: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
00115:   const s0 = safeStr(value);
00116:   if (!s0) return "";
00117: 
00118:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
00119: 
00120:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
00121:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
00122: 
00123:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
00124:   if (mISO) {
00125:     return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
00126:   }
00127: 
00128:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00129:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
00130: 
00131:   return s0;
00132: }
00133: 
00134: function hourToNum(h) {
00135:   const s = normalizeHourLike(h);
00136:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
00137:   if (!m) return -1;
00138:   return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
00139: }
00140: 
00141: function unwrapDraws(maybe) {
00142:   if (Array.isArray(maybe)) return maybe;
00143:   if (maybe && typeof maybe === "object") {
00144:     if (Array.isArray(maybe.drawsRaw)) return maybe.drawsRaw;
00145:     if (Array.isArray(maybe.draws)) return maybe.draws;
00146:     if (Array.isArray(maybe.rows)) return maybe.rows;
00147:     if (Array.isArray(maybe.data)) return maybe.data;
00148:     if (maybe.result && Array.isArray(maybe.result)) return maybe.result;
00149:   }
00150:   return [];
00151: }

00157: function normalizeBoundsResponse(b) {
00158:   const minRaw = safeStr(b?.minYmd || b?.minDate || b?.min || "");
00159:   const maxRaw = safeStr(b?.maxYmd || b?.maxDate || b?.max || "");
00160:   const minYmd = isYMD(minRaw) ? minRaw : null;
00161:   const maxYmd = isYMD(maxRaw) ? maxRaw : null;
00162:   return { minYmd, maxYmd, source: safeStr(b?.source || "") };
00163: }
00164: 
00165: function clampYmd(ymd, minYmd, maxYmd) {
00166:   const d = safeStr(ymd);
00167:   if (!isYMD(d)) return null;
00168: 
00169:   let out = d;
00170:   const min = safeStr(minYmd);
00171:   const max = safeStr(maxYmd);
00172: 
00173:   if (isYMD(min) && out < min) out = min;
00174:   if (isYMD(max) && out > max) out = max;
00175: 
00176:   return out;
00177: }
00178: 
00179: function normalizeSingleDateWithBounds(dateIn, minYmd, maxYmd) {
00180:   const d = clampYmd(dateIn, minYmd, maxYmd);
00181:   if (d) return d;
00182: 
00183:   const fallback =
00184:     clampYmd(todayYMDLocal(), minYmd, maxYmd) ||
00185:     (isYMD(maxYmd) ? maxYmd : null) ||
00186:     (isYMD(minYmd) ? minYmd : null) ||
00187:     todayYMDLocal();
00188: 
00189:   return fallback;
00190: }

00256: function isFederalInput(scope) {
00257:   const up = safeStr(scope).toUpperCase();
00258:   if (!up) return false;
00259:   if (up === SCOPE_FEDERAL) return true;
00260:   if (FEDERAL_INPUT_ALIASES.has(up)) return true;
00261:   const compact = up.replace(/[\s_]+/g, " ").trim();
00262:   if (FEDERAL_INPUT_ALIASES.has(compact)) return true;
00263:   const unders = up.replace(/[\s_]+/g, "_");
00264:   if (FEDERAL_INPUT_ALIASES.has(unders)) return true;
00265:   return false;
00266: }
00267: 
00268: function normalizeScopeInput(input) {
00269:   const s = safeStr(input).toUpperCase();
00270:   if (!s) return SCOPE_RJ;
00271: 
00272:   const compact = s.replace(/[\s_-]+/g, "");
00273: 
00274:   if (s === SCOPE_RJ || compact === "PTRIO" || compact === "RIO") {
00275:     return SCOPE_RJ;
00276:   }
00277: 
00278:   if (isFederalInput(s)) {
00279:     return SCOPE_FEDERAL;
00280:   }
00281: 
00282:   if (s === SCOPE_LOOK || compact === "LOOK" || compact === "GO") {
00283:     return SCOPE_LOOK;
00284:   }
00285: 
00286:   if (
00287:     s === SCOPE_NACIONAL ||
00288:     compact === "NACIONAL" ||
00289:     compact === "LTNACIONAL"
00290:   ) {
00291:     return SCOPE_NACIONAL;
00292:   }
00293: 
00294:   return s;
00295: }
00296: 
00297: function scopeDisplayName(scope) {
00298:   const up = normalizeScopeInput(scope);
00299: 
00300:   if (up === SCOPE_RJ) return "RIO DE JANEIRO";
00301:   if (up === SCOPE_FEDERAL) return "FEDERAL";
00302:   if (up === SCOPE_LOOK) return "LOOK";
00303:   if (up === SCOPE_NACIONAL) return "NACIONAL";
00304: 
00305:   return up;
00306: }

00312: function guessPrizeNumber(p) {
00313:   if (!p) return "";
00314: 
00315:   const direct = safeStr(p?.numero);
00316:   if (direct) return direct;
00317: 
00318:   const candidates = [
00319:     p?.milhar,
00320:     p?.milhares,
00321:     p?.m,
00322:     p?.number,
00323:     p?.num,
00324:     p?.n,
00325:     p?.value,
00326:     p?.valor,
00327:     p?.resultado,
00328:     p?.result,
00329:     p?.premio,
00330:     p?.premiation,
00331:   ];
00332: 
00333:   for (const c of candidates) {
00334:     const s = safeStr(c);
00335:     if (!s) continue;
00336:     const digits = s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
00337:     return digits || s;
00338:   }
00339: 
00340:   if (Array.isArray(p?.numbers) && p.numbers.length) {
00341:     const s = safeStr(p.numbers[0]);
00342:     const digits = s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
00343:     return digits || s;
00344:   }
00345: 
00346:   return "";
00347: }

00387:       grupo: g,

00389:     });

00391:     if (s1) return s1;
00392:   } catch {}
00393:

00395:     const a2 = getAnimalLabel(g);

00398:   } catch {}
00399:

00407: function publicBase() {
00408:   try {
00409:     const viteBase =
00410:       typeof import.meta !== "undefined" ? import.meta.env?.BASE_URL : "";
00411:     const vb = String(viteBase || "").trim();
00412:     if (vb) return vb.endsWith("/") ? vb.slice(0, -1) : vb;
00413:   } catch {}
00414: 
00415:   const b = String(process.env.PUBLIC_URL || "").trim();
00416:   return b && b !== "/" ? b : "";
00417: }
00418: 
00419: function normalizeImgSrc(src) {
00420:   const s = safeStr(src);
00421:   if (!s) return "";
00422: 
00423:   if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(s)) return s;
00424: 
00425:   const base = publicBase();
00426: 
00427:   if (s.startsWith("/")) return `${base}${s}`;
00428:   if (s.startsWith("public/")) return `${base}/${s.slice("public/".length)}`;
00429:   if (s.startsWith("img/")) return `${base}/${s}`;
00430: 
00431:   return `${base}/${s}`;
00432: }
00433: 
00434: function makeImgVariantsFromGrupo(grupo, size) {
00435:   const g = Number(grupo);
00436:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g <= 0) return [];
00437: 
00438:   const s = Number(size) || 96;
00439:   const g2 = pad2(g);
00440:   const slug = safeStr(getSlugByGrupo(g));
00441:   if (!slug) return [];
00442: 
00443:   const primary = normalizeImgSrc(getImgFromGrupo(g, s));
00444: 
00445:   const base = publicBase();
00446:   const sizedName = `${base}/assets/animals/animais_${s}_png/${g2}_${slug}_${s}.png`;
00447: 
00448:   const seeds = [primary, sizedName].filter(Boolean);
00449: 
00450:   const out = [];
00451:   for (const seed of seeds) {
00452:     const clean = String(seed).split("?")[0];
00453:     if (!clean) continue;
00454: 
00455:     out.push(clean);
00456: 
00457:     if (clean.match(/\.png$/)) out.push(clean.replace(/\.png$/, ".PNG"));
00458:     if (clean.match(/\.PNG$/)) out.push(clean.replace(/\.PNG$/, ".png"));
00459: 
00460:     out.push(clean.replace(/\.(png|PNG)$/i, ".jpg"));
00461:     out.push(clean.replace(/\.(png|PNG|jpg)$/i, ".jpeg"));
00462:   }
00463: 
00464:   return Array.from(new Set(out.filter(Boolean)));
00465: }

00467: function RowImg({ variants, alt, fallbackText }) {
00468:   const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
00469: 
00470:   if (!variants.length || failed) {
00471:     return <div className="pp_imgFallback">{fallbackText || "—"}</div>;
00472:   }
00473: 
00474:   return (
00475:     <img
00476:       className="pp_img"
00477:       src={variants[0]}
00478:       alt={alt}
00479:       loading="lazy"
00480:       data-try="0"
00481:       onError={(e) => {
00482:         const imgEl = e.currentTarget;
00483:         const i = Number(imgEl.dataset.try || "0");
00484:         const next = variants[i + 1];
00485: 
00486:         if (next) {
00487:           imgEl.dataset.try = String(i + 1);
00488:           imgEl.src = next;
00489:         } else {
00490:           setFailed(true);
00491:         }
00492:       }}
00493:     />
00494:   );
00495: }

00497: function resolveAnimalUI(prize) {
00498:   const grupo = guessPrizeGrupo(prize);
00499:   const animalRaw = guessPrizeAnimal(prize);
00500: 
00501:   if (grupo) {
00502:     const label = safeGetAnimalLabel(grupo, animalRaw);
00503:     const variants = makeImgVariantsFromGrupo(grupo, 96);
00504:     return { grupo, label, imgVariants: variants };
00505:   }
00506: 
00507:   const label = animalRaw ? animalRaw : "";
00508:   return { grupo: null, label, imgVariants: [] };
00509: }

00515: function drawKeyForDedup(d, scopeKey, ymd) {
00516:   const hour = normalizeHourLike(
00517:     d?.close_hour || d?.closeHour || d?.hour || d?.hora || ""
00518:   );
00519:   if (hour) return `HOUR:${scopeKey}|${ymd}|${hour}`;

00524: function countPrizes(d) {
00525:   return Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes.length : 0;
00526: }
00527: 
00528: function pickBetterDraw(a, b) {
00529:   const pa = countPrizes(a);
00530:   const pb = countPrizes(b);
00531:   if (pa !== pb) return pb > pa ? b : a;
00532: 
00533:   const ha = safeStr(a?.close_hour || a?.closeHour || a?.hour || a?.hora);
00534:   const hb = safeStr(b?.close_hour || b?.closeHour || b?.hour || b?.hora);
00535:   if (!!hb !== !!ha) return hb ? b : a;
00536: 
00537:   return a;
00538: }
00539: 
00540: function dedupeDraws(list, scopeKey, ymd) {
00541:   const arr = Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];
00542:   const map = new Map();
00543: 
00544:   for (const d of arr) {
00545:     const k = drawKeyForDedup(d, scopeKey, ymd);
00546:     const prev = map.get(k);
00547:     if (!prev) map.set(k, d);
00548:     else map.set(k, pickBetterDraw(prev, d));
00549:   }
00550: 
00551:   return Array.from(map.values());
00552: }

00565: function formatPrizeNumberByPos(value, pos) {
00566:   const s = safeStr(value);
00567:   if (!s) return "";

00596: function getExpectedRjHoursDesc(ymd) {
00597:   const d = ymdToDateLocal(ymd);
00598:   const dow =
00599:     d instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())
00600:       ? d.getDay()
00601:       : -1;
00602: 
00603:   let out;
00604: 
00605:   /*
00606:    * Domingo:
00607:    * a PT_RIO possui somente 14h e 16h.
00608:    */
00609:   if (dow === 0) {
00610:     return [...RJ_EXPECTED_HOURS_SUNDAY_DESC];
00611:   }
00612: 
00613:   /*
00614:    * Quarta-feira:
00615:    * não existe sorteio das 18h.
00616:    */
00617:   if (dow === 3) {
00618:     out = [...RJ_EXPECTED_HOURS_WEDNESDAY_DESC];
00619:   }
00620:   /*
00621:    * Sábado a partir de 18/07/2026:
00622:    * 19:30 substitui o antigo horário das 18h.
00623:    */
00624:   else if (
00625:     dow === 6 &&
00626:     isYMD(ymd) &&
00627:     ymd >= RJ_SATURDAY_1930_START_YMD
00628:   ) {
00629:     out = [
00630:       "21:00",
00631:       "19:30",
00632:       "16:00",
00633:       "14:00",
00634:       "11:00",
00635:     ];
00636:   } else {
00637:     out = [...RJ_EXPECTED_HOURS_REGULAR_DESC];
00638:   }
00639: 
00640:   if (
00641:     isYMD(ymd) &&
00642:     ymd >= RJ_09H_START_YMD
00643:   ) {
00644:     out.push("09:00");
00645:   }
00646: 
00647:   return out;
00648: }
00649: 
00650: function shouldShowExpectedHour(ymd, hour) {
00651:   const hNum = hourToNum(hour);
00652:   if (hNum < 0) return true;
00653: 
00654:   if (safeStr(ymd) !== todayYMDLocal()) return true;
00655: 
00656:   return hNum <= currentHourNumLocal();
00657: }
00658: 
00659: /*
00660:  * A fonte grava o novo sorteio de sábado da PT_RIO no bucket 19:00,
00661:  * embora o horário oficial exibido seja 19:30.
00662:  *
00663:  * A conversão fica restrita à camada visual da página Resultados para não
00664:  * alterar filtros, estatísticas, Top3 ou o histórico armazenado no Firestore.
00665:  */
00666: function normalizeRjDisplayHour(draw, ymd) {
00667:   const hour = normalizeHourLike(
00668:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
00669:   );
00670: 
00671:   const d = ymdToDateLocal(ymd);
00672:   const dow =
00673:     d instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())
00674:       ? d.getDay()
00675:       : -1;
00676: 
00677:   if (
00678:     hour === "19:00" &&
00679:     dow === 6 &&
00680:     isYMD(ymd) &&
00681:     ymd >= RJ_SATURDAY_1930_START_YMD
00682:   ) {
00683:     return "19:30";
00684:   }
00685: 
00686:   return hour;
00687: }

00689: function normalizeDisplayHourForScope(scopeKey, draw, ymd) {
00690:   if (scopeKey === SCOPE_NACIONAL) {
00691:     return normalizeNacionalDisplayHour(draw);
00692:   }
00693: 
00694:   if (scopeKey === SCOPE_RJ) {
00695:     return normalizeRjDisplayHour(draw, ymd);
00696:   }
00697: 
00698:   return normalizeHourLike(
00699:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
00700:   );
00701: }

00704: function normalizeNacionalDisplayHour(draw) {
00705:   const hour = normalizeHourLike(
00706:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
00707:   );
00708: 
00709:   const lotteryName = safeStr(draw?.lottery_name).toUpperCase();
00710: 
00711:   const m = lotteryName.match(/(\d{2})\s*HS?/);
00712: 
00713:   if (m) {
00714:     return `${m[1]}:00`;
00715:   }
00716: 
00717:   switch (hour) {
00718:     case "22:49": return "23:00";
00719:     case "20:49": return "21:00";
00720:     case "16:49": return "17:00";
00721:     case "14:49": return "15:00";
00722:     case "11:49": return "12:00";
00723:     case "09:49": return "10:00";
00724:     case "07:49": return "08:00";
00725:     case "01:49": return "02:00";
00726: 
00727:     case "22:50": return "23:00";
00728:     case "19:50": return "21:00";
00729:     case "16:50": return "17:00";
00730:     case "14:50": return "15:00";
00731:     case "11:50": return "12:00";
00732:     case "09:50": return "10:00";
00733:     case "07:50": return "08:00";
00734:     case "01:50": return "02:00";
00735: 
00736:     default:
00737:       return hour;
00738:   }
00739: }

00742: function buildExpectedDrawsForScope(scopeKey, orderedDraws, ymd) {
00743:   const list = Array.isArray(orderedDraws) ? orderedDraws : [];
00744:   const byHour = new Map();
00745: 
00746:   for (const d of list) {
00747:     const h = normalizeDisplayHourForScope(scopeKey, d, ymd);
00748:     if (!h) continue;
00749:     if (!byHour.has(h)) byHour.set(h, d);
00750:   }
00751: 
00752:   if (scopeKey === SCOPE_FEDERAL) {
00753:     return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
00754:       const ha = hourToNum(
00755:         a?.__slotHour || a?.close_hour || a?.closeHour || a?.hour || a?.hora
00756:       );
00757:       const hb = hourToNum(
00758:         b?.__slotHour || b?.close_hour || b?.closeHour || b?.hour || b?.hora
00759:       );
00760:       return hb - ha;
00761:     });
00762:   }
00763: 
00764:   let expectedHours = [];
00765: 
00766:   if (scopeKey === SCOPE_RJ) {
00767:     expectedHours = getExpectedRjHoursDesc(ymd);
00768:   } else if (scopeKey === SCOPE_LOOK) {
00769:     expectedHours = LOOK_EXPECTED_HOURS_DESC;
00770:   } else if (scopeKey === SCOPE_NACIONAL) {
00771:     expectedHours = NACIONAL_EXPECTED_HOURS_DESC;
00772:   }
00773: 
00774:   const visibleExpectedHours = expectedHours.filter((hour) =>
00775:     shouldShowExpectedHour(ymd, hour)
00776:   );
00777: 
00778:   const result = visibleExpectedHours.flatMap((hour) => {
00779:     const found = byHour.get(hour);
00780: 
00781:     /*
00782:      * O horário das 18h continua pertencendo ao calendário histórico da PT_RIO.
00783:      *
00784:      * Entretanto, a auditoria oficial dos sábados confirmou que, quando não
00785:      * existe resultado real às 18h, o card vazio representa uma ausência
00786:      * legítima de sorteio e não uma falha de importação.
00787:      *
00788:      * Resultados reais continuam sendo exibidos porque a busca por `found`
00789:      * acontece antes desta condição.
00790:      */
00791:     if (found) return [found];
00792: 
00793:     const selectedDate = ymdToDateLocal(ymd);
00794:     const selectedDow =
00795:       selectedDate instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(selectedDate.getTime())
00796:         ? selectedDate.getDay()
00797:         : -1;
00798: 
00799:     const isConfirmedSaturday18Absence =
00800:       scopeKey === SCOPE_RJ &&
00801:       selectedDow === 6 &&
00802:       hour === "18:00";
00803: 
00804:     if (isConfirmedSaturday18Absence) {
00805:       return [];
00806:     }
00807: 
00808:     return [
00809:       {
00810:         __placeholder: true,
00811:         __slotHour: hour,
00812:         __placeholderKind: "compact",
00813:         drawId: `placeholder_${scopeKey}_${hour}`,
00814:         id: `placeholder_${scopeKey}_${hour}`,
00815:         close_hour: hour,
00816:         closeHour: hour,
00817:         prizes: [],
00818:       },
00819:     ];
00820:   });
00821: 
00822:   const extraActual = list.filter((d) => {
00823:     const h = normalizeDisplayHourForScope(scopeKey, d, ymd);
00824: 
00825:     return h && !visibleExpectedHours.includes(h);
00826:   });
00827: 
00828:   return [...result, ...extraActual].sort((a, b) => {
00829:     const ha = hourToNum(
00830:       a?.__slotHour || a?.close_hour || a?.closeHour || a?.hour || a?.hora
00831:     );
00832:     const hb = hourToNum(
00833:       b?.__slotHour || b?.close_hour || b?.closeHour || b?.hour || b?.hora
00834:     );
00835:     return hb - ha;
00836:   });
00837: }
00838: 
00839: function monthDaysWithDraws(draws) {
00840:   const out = new Set();
00841:   for (const d of Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []) {
00842:     const y = safeStr(d?.ymd || d?.date || "");
00843:     if (isYMD(y)) out.add(y);
00844:   }
00845:   return out;
00846: }
00847: 
00848: /* =========================
00849:    Page
00850: ========================= */
00851: 
00852: export default function Results() {
00853:   const DEFAULT_SCOPE = SCOPE_RJ;
00854: 
00855:   const [scopeUi, setScopeUi] = useState(DEFAULT_SCOPE);
00856:   const [ymd, setYmd] = useState(() => todayYMDLocal());
00857: 
00858:   const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(true);
00859:   const [needsToggle, setNeedsToggle] = useState(false);
00860: 
00861:   const [calendarOpen, setCalendarOpen] = useState(false);
00862:   const [calendarMonthYmd, setCalendarMonthYmd] = useState(() =>
00863:     startOfMonthYMD(todayYMDLocal())
00864:   );
00865: 
00866:   const centerRef = useRef(null);
00867:   const calendarRef = useRef(null);
00868:   const previousScopeRef = useRef(DEFAULT_SCOPE);
00869: 
00870:   const scopeKey = useMemo(() => normalizeScopeInput(scopeUi), [scopeUi]);
00871:   const isFederal = useMemo(() => isFederalInput(scopeKey), [scopeKey]);
00872:   const label = useMemo(() => scopeDisplayName(scopeKey), [scopeKey]);
00873: 
00874:   const ymdSafe = useMemo(() => {
00875:     const s = safeStr(ymd);
00876:     return isYMD(s) ? s : todayYMDLocal();
00877:   }, [ymd]);
00878: 
00879:   const boundsQuery = useQuery({
00880:     queryKey: ["results", "bounds", scopeKey],
00881:     queryFn: async () => {
00882:       const b = await getKingBoundsByUf({ uf: scopeKey });
00883:       return normalizeBoundsResponse(b);
00884:     },
00885:     staleTime: 30 * 60 * 1000,
00886:     gcTime: 2 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
00887:     refetchOnMount: false,
00888:     refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
00889:     refetchOnReconnect: false,
00890:   });
00891: 
00892:   const effectiveBounds = useMemo(() => {
00893:     const minYmd = boundsQuery.data?.minYmd || null;
00894:     const maxYmd = boundsQuery.data?.maxYmd || null;
00895:     return { minYmd, maxYmd };
00896:   }, [boundsQuery.data?.minYmd, boundsQuery.data?.maxYmd]);
00897: 
00898:   const ymdClamped = useMemo(() => {
00899:     const minYmd = effectiveBounds?.minYmd;
00900:     const maxYmd = effectiveBounds?.maxYmd;
00901:     if (!isYMD(minYmd) && !isYMD(maxYmd)) return ymdSafe;
00902:     return normalizeSingleDateWithBounds(ymdSafe, minYmd, maxYmd);
00903:   }, [ymdSafe, effectiveBounds?.minYmd, effectiveBounds?.maxYmd]);
00904: 
00905:   const dateBR = useMemo(() => ymdToBR(ymdClamped), [ymdClamped]);
00906: 
00907:   const calendarCells = useMemo(
00908:     () => buildCalendarCells(calendarMonthYmd),
00909:     [calendarMonthYmd]
00910:   );
00911: 
00912:   const calendarTitle = useMemo(
00913:     () => monthTitleBR(calendarMonthYmd),
00914:     [calendarMonthYmd]
00915:   );
00916: 
00917:   useEffect(() => {
00918:     const previousScope = previousScopeRef.current;
00919:     const changedScope = previousScope !== scopeKey;
00920:     previousScopeRef.current = scopeKey;
00921: 
00922:     if (changedScope) {
00923:       setCalendarOpen(false);
00924:       setShowAll(true);
00925:     }
00926: 
00927:     if (!isYMD(effectiveBounds?.minYmd) && !isYMD(effectiveBounds?.maxYmd)) return;
00928: 
00929:     if (changedScope && isFederal && isYMD(effectiveBounds?.maxYmd)) {
00930:       if (ymd !== effectiveBounds.maxYmd) {
00931:         setYmd(effectiveBounds.maxYmd);
00932:       }
00933:       return;
00934:     }
00935: 
00936:     const normalized = normalizeSingleDateWithBounds(
00937:       ymdSafe,
00938:       effectiveBounds?.minYmd,
00939:       effectiveBounds?.maxYmd
00940:     );
00941: 
00942:     if (normalized && normalized !== ymd) {
00943:       setYmd(normalized);
00944:     }
00945:   }, [
00946:     scopeKey,
00947:     isFederal,
00948:     effectiveBounds?.minYmd,
00949:     effectiveBounds?.maxYmd,
00950:     ymd,
00951:     ymdSafe,
00952:   ]);
00953: 
00954:   useEffect(() => {
00955:     setCalendarMonthYmd(startOfMonthYMD(ymdClamped));
00956:   }, [ymdClamped]);
00957: 
00958:   const calendarMarksQuery = useQuery({
00959:     queryKey: ["results", "calendar-marks", scopeKey, calendarMonthYmd],
00960:     enabled:
00961:       isYMD(startOfMonthYMD(calendarMonthYmd)) &&
00962:       isYMD(endOfMonthYMD(calendarMonthYmd)),
00963:     queryFn: async () => {
00964:       const from = startOfMonthYMD(calendarMonthYmd);
00965:       const to = endOfMonthYMD(calendarMonthYmd);
00966: 
00967:       const out = await getKingResultsByRange({
00968:         uf: scopeKey,
00969:         dateFrom: from,
00970:         dateTo: to,
00971:         positions: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7],
00972:         mode: "aggregated",
00973:       });
00974: 
00975:       return Array.from(monthDaysWithDraws(out)).sort();
00976:     },
00977:     staleTime: 30 * 60 * 1000,
00978:     gcTime: 2 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
00979:     placeholderData: (prev) => prev ?? [],
00980:     refetchOnMount: false,
00981:     refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
00982:     refetchOnReconnect: false,
00983:   });
00984: 
00985:   const dayResultsQuery = useQuery({
00986:     queryKey: ["results", "day", scopeKey, ymdClamped],
00987:     enabled: !!safeStr(scopeKey) && isYMD(ymdClamped),
00988:     queryFn: async () => {
00989:       const sKey = safeStr(scopeKey);
00990:       const d = safeStr(ymdClamped);
00991: 
00992:       const out = await getKingResultsByDate({
00993:         uf: sKey,
00994:         date: d,
00995:         closeHour: null,
00996:         closeHourBucket: null,
00997:         positions: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7],
00998:       });
00999: 
01000:       const list = unwrapDraws(out);
01001:       const deduped = dedupeDraws(list, sKey, d);
01002: 
01003:       console.log("[RESULTS_DEBUG]", {
01004:         scope: sKey,
01005:         date: d,
01006:         rawLen: Array.isArray(out) ? out.length : null,
01007:         listLen: Array.isArray(list) ? list.length : null,
01008:         dedupedLen: Array.isArray(deduped) ? deduped.length : null,
01009:         hours: deduped.map((x) => ({
01010:           id: x?.drawId || x?.id || null,

01012:           ymd: x?.ymd || x?.date || null,

01015:           placeholder: !!x?.__placeholder,
01016:         })),
01017:       });
01018: 
01019:       return deduped;
01020:     },
01021:     staleTime: ymdClamped === todayYMDLocal() ? 30 * 1000 : 10 * 60 * 1000,
01022:     gcTime: 2 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
01023:     placeholderData: (prev) => prev ?? [],
01024: 
01025:     // Resultados do dia atual podem entrar depois que a tela já foi aberta.
01026:     // Faz nova leitura periódica somente para hoje, sem gerar consultas
01027:     // desnecessárias ao navegar por datas históricas.
01028:     refetchInterval:
01029:       ymdClamped === todayYMDLocal()
01030:         ? 60 * 1000
01031:         : false,
01032:     refetchIntervalInBackground: false,
01033: 
01034:     // Ao retornar para a tela ou recuperar a conexão, busca o estado real.
01035:     refetchOnMount: true,
01036:     refetchOnWindowFocus: true,
01037:     refetchOnReconnect: true,
01038:   });
01039: 
01040:   const loading = dayResultsQuery.isLoading && !Array.isArray(dayResultsQuery.data);
01041: 
01042:   const error = safeStr(
01043:     dayResultsQuery.error?.message ||
01044:       boundsQuery.error?.message ||

01048:

01051:   }, [dayResultsQuery.data]);
01052: 
01053:   const calendarMarkedYmds = useMemo(() => {

01056:

01063:     calendarMarksQuery.isFetching && !Array.isArray(calendarMarksQuery.data);
01064: 
01065:   useEffect(() => {
01066:     function onDocPointerDown(e) {
01067:       if (!calendarOpen) return;
01068:       if (calendarRef.current && !calendarRef.current.contains(e.target)) {
01069:         setCalendarOpen(false);
01070:       }
01071:     }
01072: 
01073:     function onDocKeyDown(e) {
01074:       if (e.key === "Escape") {
01075:         setCalendarOpen(false);
01076:       }

01078: 
01079:     document.addEventListener("mousedown", onDocPointerDown);
01080:     document.addEventListener("keydown", onDocKeyDown);
01081: 
01082:     return () => {
01083:       document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onDocPointerDown);
01084:       document.removeEventListener("keydown", onDocKeyDown);
01085:     };
01086:   }, [calendarOpen]);
01087:

01089:     setShowAll(true);

01091: 
01092:   const drawsOrdered = useMemo(() => {
01093:     const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
01094:     return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
01095:       const ha = hourToNum(a?.close_hour || a?.closeHour || a?.hour || a?.hora);
01096:       const hb = hourToNum(b?.close_hour || b?.closeHour || b?.hour || b?.hora);
01097:       if (ha !== hb) return hb - ha;
01098: 
01099:       const la = safeStr(a?.lottery_code || a?.lotteryCode || "");
01100:       const lb = safeStr(b?.lottery_code || b?.lotteryCode || "");
01101:       if (la !== lb) return lb.localeCompare(la);
01102: 
01103:       const ia = safeStr(a?.drawId || a?.id || "");
01104:       const ib = safeStr(b?.drawId || b?.id || "");
01105:       return ib.localeCompare(ia);
01106:     });
01107:   }, [draws]);
01108: 
01109:   const drawsDisplayBase = useMemo(() => {
01110:     return buildExpectedDrawsForScope(scopeKey, drawsOrdered, ymdClamped);
01111:   }, [scopeKey, drawsOrdered, ymdClamped]);
01112: 
01113:   useEffect(() => {

01115:   }, [drawsDisplayBase.length]);
01116: 
01117:   const drawsForView = useMemo(() => {
01118:     if (!drawsDisplayBase.length) return [];

01120:     if (showAll) return drawsDisplayBase;
01121:     return drawsDisplayBase.slice(0, 6);
01122:   }, [drawsDisplayBase, needsToggle, showAll]);
01123: 
01124:   const canGoPrevMonth = useMemo(() => {
01125:     if (!isYMD(effectiveBounds?.minYmd)) return true;

01128:       startOfMonthYMD(effectiveBounds.minYmd)

01131: 
01132:   const canGoNextMonth = useMemo(() => {
01133:     if (!isYMD(effectiveBounds?.maxYmd)) return true;
01134:     return (
01135:       shiftMonthYMD(calendarMonthYmd, 1) <=
01136:       startOfMonthYMD(effectiveBounds.maxYmd)
01137:     );
01138:   }, [calendarMonthYmd, effectiveBounds?.maxYmd]);
01139:

01143:       nextYmd,
01144:       effectiveBounds?.minYmd,

01148:     setCalendarOpen(false);
01149:   }
